08  附件档案 / Attached Files

A Tea-Scented Letter
携有隐约茶香的信件

Dear Vertin,
    I hope this letter finds you well.
    I'm sorry I won't able to see you off—things have piled up due to factional disputes within the Foundation. We live in such chaos that even an organization as self-professedly rational as the Foundation cannot avoid being swept into the political whirlpool. I wish you safe travels to the Eastern Front.
    I've enclosed a copy of Commander Ivan Ivanovich Burakov's file. You're about to enter the jurisdiction of the Zeno Cavalry Corps. Ivan and Igor have known each other since childhood. Both come from an old village downstream of the Don, yet fate drove them apart along the same muddy roads. They once commanded two separate units within Zeno, nominally independent but with an unspoken understanding that lingered between them.
    Commander Ivan is difficult to gauge. According to the records, he's plainspoken and awkward in social settings, which has earned him Admiral Somme's high praise as a "loyal and trustworthy" man.
    However, I must caution you: don't place complete trust in him. Hearts rust in wartime, especially those of soldiers who've endured betrayal and hard choices. Moreover, the bond between Ivan and Igor may pose hidden risks. In times like these, you'll have to weigh trust and suspicion for yourself.
    Winter is coming to the Don. The damp cold settles heavily over riverside cities this time of year. Along with the file, I've included several packets of ginger tea. Steep it in hot water and drink—it helps ward off the chill.
    Stay in touch if you can.
    Safe travels.
Zhang Zhizhi
November 25, 1920
致维尔汀，
    展信安。
    近日因基金会内部的派系纷争，事务堆叠如山，未能与你见临行前的一面。时局之乱，连基金会这般自诩理性的组织，也难免被卷入政治的漩涡。希望你在东线的行程一路顺风。
    随信附上伊万·伊万诺维奇·布列科夫军长的档案复印件。你即将进入芝诺骑兵军的管辖区域，而伊万与伊戈尔自幼便有交情，二人同样出身于顿河下游的旧村庄，命运却让他们在同一片泥泞中分道扬镳。两人曾分别领导芝诺内部的两支部队，名义上各行其是，实则仍有若隐若现的默契。
    伊万军长是一位难以简单评判的军人。根据记录，他作风朴实，不擅应付社交，但也因此得到了索姆将军对其“忠实守信”的高度评价。
    不过，我仍要提醒你：不要对他抱有百分之百的信任。人心在战争中是会生锈的，特别是经历过背叛与抉择的军人。何况，伊万与伊戈尔之间的情谊也可能埋下隐患。此种情形下，你需要在信任与怀疑之间做出你自己的判断。
    顿河一带即将入冬，河畔城市的冬天更易引来湿寒。除档案外，我还在随信附带了几包姜茶，热水浸泡服用，可驱散寒邪。
    若情况允许，请定期联络。
    此致，祝一路顺风。
                                                             张之之
                                                             1920年11月25日

09  寂静所笼罩的 / Beneath the Silence

Beneath the Silence
寂静所笼罩的

Riverside, Steppe
草原河畔

Sergey: Hey! I got something! Sasha, come give me a hand!
谢尔盖：嘿，我捞着东西了！快过来帮把手，萨沙！

Not far from the camp, Sergey has been watering the horses while he fishes, undeterred by the danger brewing across the steppe.
在距离营地不远的河边，出来为连队饮马的谢尔盖仍在坚持不懈地捞鱼——草原上酝酿的危险还没有吓退他。

The fishnet he failed to steal last year ended up in his pack before he left home. Old Somov put it there himself.
去年没能偷到手的渔网，在离乡时还是出现在了谢尔盖的行囊里，老索莫夫亲手装进去的。

Zeno Cavalryman I: Got it! Dear Lord, this is heavy!
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：我抓住了——该死的，这网真沉。

Sergey: Must be a big one. Come on, Sasha, put your back into it.
谢尔盖：这证明我们捞到真正的大东西了，快，使点劲啊，萨沙！

The two young men, children of this very river, use all their strength to drag the fishnet to the riverbank.
两个在河畔长大的青年用尽全身的力气，才刚刚足够将激流中的渔网缓缓拖向岸边。

Zeno Cavalryman I: Have you ever caught a fish this big before?
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：谢尔盖，你见过力气这么大的鱼吗？

Sergey: Never! The fish done all the growing this year instead of the crops?
谢尔盖：从来没有，地里的麦子不长了，鱼倒是在疯长。

Sergey: Just a bit further. Grab tight. On my mark. One! Two! Three!
谢尔盖：就差一点，别废话了，你抓好网的另一头，听我的——一，二，三！

They stumble back as they finally haul the net ashore.
捕鱼者向后栽倒，而渔网里的重物也终于被拖上了岸。

Zeno Cavalryman I: Go check it out. I dare say even my grandpa has never seen fish so big.
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：快去看看，这到底是什么东西——我打赌，我爷爷这辈子都没见过这种鱼。

Sergey runs to the net and untangles the cords like an expert.
谢尔盖首先跑到网边，手法熟练地解开纠缠的网绳。

???: *cough*
？？？：咳……

That's not a sound a fish would make.
怎么听这都不像是鱼类能发出来的声音。

Sergey: Huh? Thi-This is—
谢尔盖：啊？这是，这——

???: *cough*
？？？：咳——咳！

Zeno Cavalryman I: What kind of fish is it, Sergey?
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：到底是什么鱼啊，谢尔盖？

Sasha hurries over to join him.
伙伴还在远处，正揣着巨大的好奇心往谢尔盖身边赶来。

Sergey: I'll be damned! Sasha! It's a person?
谢尔盖：我的老天爷，萨沙，这、这是一个人？！

Zeno Cavalryman I: A person? Have you lost your mind?
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：一个人？你疯了吧！

???: *cough* Help. Help me.
？？？：咳、咳咳！救……救命……

Sergey: Damn it! Hurry up, will you? It's a living person! We have to take her to camp!
谢尔盖：快来，该死的，这真的是一个人！她还活着，快把她抬到营地里去！

Merel: Hmm?
梅蕾尔：嗯……？

The sound of dozens of horses neighing at once echoes from somewhere far away.
她听见马匹的鸣叫，不过不是一匹，而是数十匹马在同时出声，而且听起来离她十分遥远。

Merel: Andreas? Charon? Where *cough* where am I?
梅蕾尔：……安德烈森……卡戎？——这是，咳咳，这是哪里？

Warm, dry air sweeps across her cheeks. She hears the crackle of burning willow sticks.
温暖干燥的气流吹向她的面颊，她听见柳木条燃烧的噼啪声。

Sergey: You're awake? Try getting to your feet.
谢尔盖：醒了？站起来试试。

Merel: Ergh. My head's spinning. I can hardly ...
梅蕾尔：呕……我的头好晕，站不起来。

Sergey: Whoa, whoa, easy now. Just try and take a few steps. It'll help, trust me.
谢尔盖：忍住，别倒下，尽量走两步，这对溺水的人很有用，相信我。

Merel doesn't know how she gets to her feet. All she remembers is swaying as she somehow pulled herself upright.
梅蕾尔完全不知道自己是怎么站起来的，她只记得自己摇摇晃晃、不明不白地站直了身子。

Merel: I ... What is this place?
梅蕾尔：我……这是……这是哪里？

Sergey: Relax. You're in the Zeno Cavalry Corps camp. It's safe here.
谢尔盖：芝诺骑兵军营地。放心，你很安全。

Merel: Z-Zeno? Oh, you've got to be kidding.
梅蕾尔：芝……芝诺？不是吧……

That name is like a hell-bent killer pursuing her to the ends of the earth.
这个名字就像一个熟悉的杀手，似乎要追杀她到世界的尽头。

Sergey doesn't notice the unusual panic that grips her. To him, she's simply a frightened lost girl.
谢尔盖没有意识到落难少女语气中不自然的惊恐。对他来说，她多半只是吓坏了。

Merel looks around and sees a soldier in a different uniform walking toward her.
梅蕾尔四下张望，看见一位着装明显不同的士兵向她走来。

Lilya: Sergey, I heard you found a girl.
红弩箭：你把什么人带来了，谢尔盖？

Merel: No. It can't be ...
梅蕾尔：这……这不是……

Sergey: We fished her out of the river, Lieutenant. She just woke up.
谢尔盖：从河里捞上来的，中尉，她刚醒。

Lilya: And you just brought her straight into camp? She's obviously not a local. What if she's a rebel spy, huh?
红弩箭：喔？你怎么知道不是叛军的探子？——她看起来可不像本地人啊。

Sergey: Sure, she's not a local, but she doesn't seem like a bandit either.
谢尔盖：是不像本地人，但这样子也不像匪军的人吧。

Lilya steps closer and stares at her for a long while.
红弩箭走近梅蕾尔，盯着她看了好一阵。

Lilya: Hm. Fair enough. What's your name?
红弩箭：……也有道理。你叫什么名字？

Merel: I—
梅蕾尔：我——

She falters before she can say the false name she created. Under Lieutenant Lilya's piercing gaze, the girl loses the courage to lie.
一个刚编好的假名字被她吓得咽了回去。在红弩箭中尉的质问下，少女失去了撒谎的勇气。

Merel: M-Merel.
梅蕾尔：梅……梅蕾尔。

Lilya: Merel? Not from around here, then. How'd you end up in the water?
红弩箭：梅蕾尔？好吧，听起来确实不是本地人。你是怎么掉水里的？

Merel: I, uh, I came seeking refuge near the river, and then, I ... I was chased by some people—bandits or something.
梅蕾尔：我是逃难到这儿来的，呃……然后，就是……在河边碰到了也许是土匪之类的人。

Merel: I tried to escape, but I fell off my horse and into the river.
梅蕾尔：我开始跑，但是不小心从马背上跌下来，摔进了河里。

Hearing her tale of misfortune, Lilya shakes her head helplessly and turns to Sergey.
红弩箭无可奈何地摇头，为少女的不幸而遗憾。她转向谢尔盖。

Lilya: Send her somewhere safe after she recovers. We'll decamp tomorrow morning, station in Tamarovka for a day, and then head to the city. Commander's orders.
红弩箭：等她恢复好之后就送她到安全的地方去。军长下令了，明天一早拔营进城，中途在红柳树村驻扎一日。

Sergey: Really? That's my home! I can visit my aunt and uncle! I can sleep in my own bed for a night!
谢尔盖：真的？那就是我家呀，我要住自己的房子去，看看姑父和姑母。

Lilya: You'll have to apply for leave to do that. You eaten anything, Merel? We still got some canned meat from the Foundation.
红弩箭：你得向军长请假才行——对了，梅蕾尔，我去给你拿些吃的，基金会送来的肉罐头还剩了些，你刚从水里出来，可不能再喝白菜汤了。

Merel: Canned meat ...
梅蕾尔：肉罐头……

The temptation of canned meat outweighs all fear of the Foundation. She swallows her saliva.
“基金会”一词带来的压迫感被“肉罐头”的诱惑所取代。饥肠辘辘的少女咽下一口唾沫。

After the lieutenant leaves, Sergey returns to his usual complaining.
中尉离开后，谢尔盖照常开始抱怨起来。

Sergey: Too bad I only caught you today, huh?
谢尔盖：可惜啊，今天没捞着什么鱼，光把你捞上来了。

Merel: Um, s-sorry?
梅蕾尔：那……对、对不起？

Sergey: Don't worry. It's not every day you fish a person out of the river, hahaha. Oh, by the way, you can stay at my uncle's when we arrive in Tamarovka if you like.
谢尔盖：别担心，以后还有机会捞鱼，总不至于再捞上个人来。对了，到了红柳树村，你也可以直接住进我姑父的家。

Sergey: It's no Winter Palace, but it's better than a tent, that's for sure.
谢尔盖：不是什么好房子，但总比住在帐篷里舒服。

Merel: You live with your uncle?
梅蕾尔：你和你姑父一起生活？

Sergey: Yeah. Father died at the front four years ago, and my mother was sick—lost her two years later. My aunt and uncle took me in.
谢尔盖：是啊，我老爹四年前死在前线了，妈妈在前年冬天也病死了，好在姑姑一家人好，收留了我。

Sergey: I do have to do farm work to earn my keep, but that's no big deal.
谢尔盖：当然，我们得替他们家干农活，但这对我来说不算什么问题。

Sergey: How about you? Where are you from? You said you're a refugee. Where's your family now?
谢尔盖：你是从哪儿来的？——你说你是逃难的，你的家人现在在哪里？

Merel: I ... I ...
梅蕾尔：我……我……

The girl stares at the empty ground before her.
少女双目失神地盯着眼前的空地。

Merel: I don't have a family.
梅蕾尔：我没有家人。

Even the ever-slippery Sergey loses his smile. He can't help but think of that winter when his mother lay dying.
一向不正经的谢尔盖也收起了笑意，他不禁回想起母亲在咳嗽声中死去的那个冬天。

Merel: An, um, orphanage took me in. I grew up there.
梅蕾尔：一个，嗯……孤儿学校收留了我，之后我就在学校里长大……

Merel: Are there many locals in the cavalry? Like you?
梅蕾尔：骑兵军里有很多本地人吗，像你一样？

Sergey: Yeah, most of us actually. Back when Zeno first got here, there were only a thousand people. The Cavalry Corps didn't even exist.
谢尔盖：没错，像我一样当兵的大部分都是本地人。芝诺刚到这里来的时候只有千来号人，还算不上什么骑兵军呢。

Sergey: When the famine struck the steppe, and even potatoes wouldn't grow, the cavalry gave us food and shelter.
谢尔盖：后来顿河开始闹饥荒，地里连土豆都长不出来了。但骑兵军给吃给住，也不乱来。

Sergey: So I thought, maybe it wouldn't be too bad to join them. At least then I could save some food for my family.
谢尔盖：我觉得来给他们当兵也不错，至少能给家里多留些粮食。

Merel: But you've enlisted in an army. You'll have to fight.
梅蕾尔：可……这是入伍，你要上战场的。

Sergey: But I haven't had to yet. And everyone's alive and fitter than ever. Isn't that enough?
谢尔盖：这不还没开始正经打仗嘛，眼下大家都还活得好好的，这不就成了？

Lilya: Friends already, huh?
红弩箭：你们已经是朋友了？

She precisely tosses a can of meat into Merel's arms.
红弩箭从物资库慢悠悠地走回来，把一个肉罐头精准地扔进了梅蕾尔怀里。

Sergey: Nothing for me, Lieutenant?
谢尔盖：没有我的份吗，中尉？

Lilya: Too slow. Got to get it yourself now.
红弩箭：嘴馋了不早说？自己去领！

Merel, silent among their chatter, stares at the Foundation symbol on the can for a long while.
梅蕾尔藏在芝诺士兵们轻松的闲聊中，低头盯着罐头上基金会的标志看了许久。

Finally, she slips her index finger under the ring and pulls the can open. She lowers her head and sips the oily gravy.
最终，她用食指拉开了罐头拉环，低头啜饮了一口飘着油花的肉汁。

???: There was a time I thought you would lead us to the eternal. I thought you were like me—a man with ambition, a leader worth following.
？？？：曾经，我以为你会带领我们夺取永恒之物。我以为你像我一样，拥有这种崇高的、值得人追随的野心。

???: But look what you've become. A coward.
？？？：可你现在变成了什么？——我从没想过，你是一个临阵退缩的懦夫。

???: You buckled while I stood tall. I have far excelled you, Igor, and the world will worship me for it.
？？？：我的立场从来没有变过，我要登上的是受人供奉的祭坛。在这一点上，我远超过你，伊戈尔。

He whips out his pistol, aims at the tall figure blocking his way, and pulls the trigger, only to hear the faint click of the firing pin.
他拿出手枪，瞄准眼前拦路的高大身影，然后扣下扳机，却只听见撞针击空时发出的脆弱“咔哒”声。

Igor: No mortal can transcend eternity, Krutov.
伊戈尔：……没有凡人能跨越永恒，格鲁托夫。

His opponent calmly draws his shashka.
反倒是对手从容不迫地拔出了马刀。

Igor: Your arrogance has gone beyond your means, turning you cruel and foolish. How disappointing.
伊戈尔：你的傲慢超越了你应有的限度，使你变得愚蠢而残忍。这令我失望。

Krutov: You can't kill me, Igor. You—
格鲁托夫：你杀不死我，伊戈尔，你——

Krutov: ...!!
格鲁托夫：——！

Shouts, gunfire, curses, as men shove past one another and hooves kick up an almost scalding dust.
喧哗。枪声。互相推搡时的咒骂。马蹄掀起几乎有些烫人的尘埃。

Escort Soldier: Bring everyone here! Separate the arcanists from the humans! Zeno's captives in their own line! Move it, slackers!
押送士兵：——把人都带过来，神秘学家和人类都分开。芝诺的俘虏单独带到一边，快动起来，一群懒鬼！

Krutov: ...
格鲁托夫：……

He wakes from his restless dreams. Incense still lingers in the air around him, the hateful scent of henbane clinging to his nostrils.
他从一贯焦躁的梦境中脱离，周身仍然弥漫着残余的熏香，令人憎恶的天仙子气味挥之不去。

Tent, Krutov's Encampment
格鲁托夫营地·营帐

Krutov: Useless. I knew that witless ritualist was not to be trusted.
格鲁托夫：没用的东西……我就知道那些脑子烧坏的术士完全不可靠。

Ritualist: General Krutov.
仪式术士：格鲁托夫将军。

As if having foreseen that the man in the tent would awake, an oddly dressed ritualist slips inside.
似乎是早已预料到营帐中的人已清醒，一位着装与四周环境格格不入的术士侧身进入。

Ritualist: You have awoken, yet the incense has yet to fade. I suggest you return to your dreams until you're capable of overcoming your nightmare.
仪式术士：熏香还未完全散去，你就已经清醒了。依我之见，你应该继续梦境，直到你能战胜梦魇。

Krutov: Enough of your riddles and trickery. Why are you here?
格鲁托夫：这些谜语你爱说多少就说多少，但我已经受够这种把戏了——你进来做什么？

Ritualist: Captives. The first batch has arrived.
仪式术士：俘虏。第一批俘虏已经送到了营地。

Krutov: Then you do as we agreed: Take the arcanists and leave the rest to me. What part of that do you not understand?
格鲁托夫：那就按照我们说好的去做，你把神秘学家带走，其余人都留下来交给我处置。有什么问题吗？

Ritualist: There is a peculiar individual among the captives—an Awakened. We didn't discuss how to deal with them.
仪式术士：被俘的人中有一个比较奇特的个体，一名意识唤醒者。我们之前没有讨论过如何处理这种东西。

Krutov: An Awakened. Those can be particularly useful. What do you know of it?
格鲁托夫：意识唤醒者通常有些不同寻常的用处。关于它，你还知道些什么？

Ritualist: Thanks to the knowledge bestowed by Her, I was able to glean some insight. Though, I'm surprised to have encountered it here.
仪式术士：拜祂赐予的知识，我得以知晓一些有关它的情报。没想到会在这里见到它。

Ritualist: It seems the human fools named it after a mythological deity, as if by doing so, it would somehow render it capable of resting their sinful souls in peace.
仪式术士：一些可悲而愚钝的人采用神话中的名字为它命名，仿佛靠这种方法，就能让他们有罪的灵魂安息……

Krutov: Interesting. Take me to it.
格鲁托夫：有意思，带我去看看。

Tent, Krutov's Encampment
格鲁托夫营地·营帐

Between the thudding of sandbags hitting the ground, bursts of gunfire echo through the camp.
营地边缘传来间歇性的沙袋落地声，每一次间隔中都夹杂着一阵密集的枪响。

Charon: ...
卡戎：……

Having already given his life to the tragedy of this age, Charon has already sensed what is happening around him.
已为这个时代的悲剧献出生命的亡者，凭借着本能，感知周遭正在发生的一切。

He wants to leave but cannot move. Not long ago, a ritualist scratched a series of symbols beneath his feet, binding him to the spot.
他想要离开这处困境，却始终动弹不得。不久前，一位术士在他脚下留下了一连串能禁锢行动的符号。

Krutov: Charon, is it?
格鲁托夫：卡戎，是吗？

He senses the approaching footsteps only when they are already close, then turns toward the voice.
他很晚才意识到迫近的脚步声。顺着声音的来向，他缓缓转过身。

Charon: Why did you kill those who surrendered?
卡戎：……为什么要杀死那些投降的人？

Krutov: Well, too many mouths to feed. There isn't enough food across the entire Don to keep everyone alive through the winter.
格鲁托夫：我没有那么多粮食来喂他们，事实上，整个顿河也没有足够的粮食供现在的所有人过冬。

Charon: You could have set them free. They were only civilians. They would cause you no trouble.
卡戎：你可以把他们放走，他们只是平民，不会对你的军队造成伤害。

Krutov: Civilian implies civilization—civility, wouldn't you say? The Cossacks are barbarians. Setting them free would only bolster my enemies. I will not take the risk.
格鲁托夫：草原上只有凶狠而野蛮的哥萨克，没有平民。这些人会去投靠我的敌人，我不想好心做了坏事。

Charon: ...
卡戎：……

Charon lowers his head in silence. It seems there are no words left in him.
亡者垂首沉默，看起来他要说的、能说的已经说完。

Krutov: Intriguing. Just as the ritualist said. Tell me, are you able to foresee a man's death?
格鲁托夫：真有意思……你和那个术士说得一模一样。告诉我，你真的能看见人们的死亡吗？

Charon: The shadow of death lingers over these grasslands. Countless will die in a killing driven by starvation and hatred. That is what brought me here.
卡戎：我知道阴影已经笼罩了草原，将有无数人死于饥饿与仇恨驱使的杀戮。所以，我来到了这里。

Krutov looks off to the edge of the camp, where the yellow pasture grass bows gently on small rises.
格鲁托夫扭过头，看向营地边缘，微微隆起的小丘上，枯黄的牧草温顺地低垂着。

Gunfire crackles again, along with the sound of something crashing to the ground.
又有一阵密集的枪声，同样地伴随着某些东西摔向地面的声音。

Krutov: A shadow, you say? I see a man walking the grasslands, the dead nourishing the soil. He stands amid a bountiful harvest. I am that man.
格鲁托夫：就连我也能告诉你，草原即将被鲜血灌溉。明年的土地将会更加丰沃，而我是主宰这一切的主人。

Krutov: Your "shadow of death" means nothing. What I need is a precise prophecy, one for a single man, for me.
格鲁托夫：我需要的不是这个，而是一个更精准的死亡预言，关于某一个人，或者……关于我自己。

Charon: I cannot do such a thing. Nor is there any desire to.
卡戎：我无法做到这样的事，也不愿预言任何人的死亡。

Krutov: I am offering you a chance, Awakened. Serve me and my army, and I will spare you the ritualist's hand.
格鲁托夫：我是在给你机会，意识唤醒者。留在我身边，为我的军队服务，我就不会把你交到那个术士手上。

Charon: Ritualist?
卡戎：术士？

Krutov gestures toward the binding ritual beneath Charon with his chin.
格鲁托夫用下巴指了指卡戎脚踩着的禁锢术式。

Krutov: This is her work. She tells me you dispelled another incantation cast by her kind on a human soldier.
格鲁托夫：这就是那个术士的杰作。她告诉我，你曾经用神秘术消除了这些术士施加在一个人类身上的精神咒术。

Krutov: She's desperate to tear you apart and see how you did it. Some special arcane skill, she claims.
格鲁托夫：现在，他们非常希望把你一点点拆成碎片，搞清楚你的神秘术究竟是如何运作的。

Krutov: Trust me. What she will do to you is one thing you do not want to foresee.
格鲁托夫：相信我，那些疯子不会让你好受的。

Charon: Manus Vindictae.
卡戎：……重塑之手。

Krutov: Ah, so you can see some specifics. Well? What is your decision?
格鲁托夫：看来你也不是真的一无所知。现在，你知道该怎么做决定了吗？

Charon: No. Neither your enemy nor your ally. There is nothing I can do for you but bury the dead.
卡戎：不……我既不是你的敌人，也不是你的盟友。除了为死者送葬，我无法为你们做到任何事。

Krutov fixes a sharp gaze on Charon, as if he could tear through the cloth covering his face with sight alone.
格鲁托夫凝视着卡戎，眼色锐利，仿佛是想切开对方遮蔽面容的血污白布。

Krutov: Difference of opinion, hmph?
格鲁托夫：呵——理念不合，是吗？

Krutov: Why am I surprised? You're just like the rest of them. One bad decision after another!
格鲁托夫：人们总是这样，做出对所有人都不好的决定，所有人——所有人都在做错误的事！

His cursing seems to be directed at some invisible onlooker rather than at Charon.
比起卡戎，他简直是在咒骂一个并不存在于此处的对象。

Krutov: And I am left to right their wrongs. It seems only I can.
格鲁托夫：而我会纠正所有的错误，只有我才会做到。

Krutov: I gave you your chance, freak.
格鲁托夫：我给过你机会了，怪东西。

10  陌生的目光 / Strange Gazes

Strange Gazes
陌生的目光

Thousands of warhorses carrying fully armed cavalrymen travel to Tamarovka, their heavy hooves pounding the dirt road into hard-packed earth.
数千匹战马走过红柳树村前的道路，它们驮着全副武装的骑兵，用沉重的马蹄将土路踏得坚硬无比。

The Cavalry Corps's reconnaissance team reaches the village first. The villagers are on alert, eyeing the soldiers from their doorways.
骑兵军侦察连队首先抵达了村庄。村民们在屋门前张望，以一种警惕的姿态看着骑兵军士兵。

Tamarovka
红柳树村

Sergey: You heard, Merel? A Foundation big shot's coming to the village. That's a first!
谢尔盖：你听说了吗，梅蕾尔？等会儿会有个基金会的老爷到村里来。我们村还是第一次见这种人。

Merel: Big shot? Who?
梅蕾尔：老爷？什么老爷？

Merel unconsciously reaches for her mask, suddenly realizing that she lost it during her escape.
梅蕾尔不自觉地想拉起面罩，陡然意识到她已经在逃难的路上弄丢了原来的衣服。

Sergey: Not sure, but the commander needs to welcome them personally. *sigh* Forget it. None of our business.
谢尔盖：听说是个大官，军长亲自接待……算了，这不关我们的事。

Sergey: Natash—huh?
谢尔盖：娜塔——什么？

As they pass a nearby house, Sergey seems to spot an old acquaintance in a doorway. He leans forward to greet her, but she shuts the door in his face.
当他们走近一栋房屋时，谢尔盖似乎看见了熟人，想靠过去打声招呼。站在房门口的女人却飞快地关上了门。

The hostility radiating from the onlookers in every direction makes Merel want to curl into a ball.
梅蕾尔再也无法忽视从四面八方袭来的敌意，这种被盯着看的感觉让她想要缩成一团。

Merel: Um, Sergey, isn't this your hometown? Why are they staring at you like that?
梅蕾尔：……谢尔盖，这真是你原来的村子吗？为什么村子里的人看我们的眼神这么奇怪。

Sergey: I don't know. It's not like I've made that many enemies. Just one or two at most.
谢尔盖：我可不记得我之前有过这么多仇家，顶多……顶多也就一两个。这是在搞什么？

Sergey: Anyway, let's go meet my uncle. See that house with the iron sheeting? That's our home.
谢尔盖：搞不明白，还是去见我姑父吧——看见那座铁皮瓦的房子了吗？那就是我家。

Merel follows his finger to a house with a blue iron-sheet roof. A man holding a cane stands in the yard.
梅蕾尔顺着谢尔盖指示的方向看去，在那座蓝色铁皮屋顶房子的院落前，见到了一位拄着拐杖的老人。

Sergey takes off his hat and excitedly waves it in the air.
谢尔盖兴奋地摘下帽子，举起来不断挥舞。

Sergey: Uncle Petro!
谢尔盖：姑父！

The old man only strikes the ground with his cane. His heavy blink seems to say, "Don't start, boy."
老人止不住地用拐杖在地上敲着，拼命挤着眼睛，似乎在说“臭小子别开口了”。

Sergey: What's going on?
谢尔盖：奇怪……

Merel: Maybe something happened? Should we go ask him?
梅蕾尔：也许有什么隐情……要不我们还是靠过去问一下吧。

Sergey never expected his return home to be so dispirited. His uncle pulls him aside. Merel follows behind.
谢尔盖从没想到自己会一脸沮丧地来到家门口。姑父一把将养子拉到一边，梅蕾尔也下意识地跟了上去。

Sergey: What's wrong, Uncle Petro?
谢尔盖：这到底是在干什么呀？

Sergey's Uncle: You tell me! Did your commander say you'd fight those bandits or not?
姑父：还问我呢，倒是我要问你小子——你们的军长到底打不打那帮匪军？

Sergey: Of course!
谢尔盖：当然是要去打的。

Sergey's Uncle: When?
姑父：什么时候打？

Sergey: That-That I don't know. It's a military secret!
谢尔盖：这、这倒是没告诉我……行动时间可是军事机密！

Sergey's Uncle: Word is you're going to the city—the lot of you! So where does that leave the rest of us, hm? The villagers need protecting too!
姑父：有人说你们要进城去当乌龟，那我们这些住在乡下的，骑兵军还管不管了？

The young man can't think of an answer. He can only stand in awkward silence. The uncle sighs and grips his nephew's shoulder hard with a slim yet powerful hand.
青年人没有想好怎么回答，只好尴尬地沉默着。姑父叹一口气，用一只干瘦有力的手死死捉住谢尔盖的胳膊。

Sergey's Uncle: Alright, Seryozha, I understand. Everything's packed and ready to go. Come, we're leaving to your cousin's in Petrovka.
姑父：我懂了，我们一起逃难去吧。家里的东西已经收拾好了，今天我们就走，投奔你姑母在彼得罗夫卡的表姐去。

Sergey: Uncle Petro! Could you just tell me what's going on? I'm not deserting Zeno!
谢尔盖：姑父，你能不能把话说清楚一点！我怎么能当逃兵呢？

Sergey shakes off his uncle's hand. The old man's neck flushes red with embarrassment and anger. He turns and glances at Merel.
谢尔盖甩开姑父的手。一阵尴尬的愠怒染红了老人的脖子，他别过脸，斜眼看向一旁的梅蕾尔。

Sergey: It's fine. She's a friend.
谢尔盖：她是我朋友，你别在意她，说吧。

Sergey's Uncle: The cavalry is leaving us here to die. And the refugees from upstream—they said there are tens of thousands of bandits up there.
姑父：我们都在说，骑兵军不会管我们死活了，就算要管也管不了了。上游逃难过来的人说，匪军有好几万呢。

Sergey's Uncle: I don't know where they all came from, but your cavalry's nothing compared to that, right? You'll never fight them all off.
姑父：他们哪儿来这么多人啊？——这我倒不知道了，反正比你们骑兵军的人还多不少吧。你们拿什么打呢？

Sergey's Uncle: And keep this quiet, but the kolduny left and joined the bandits!
姑父：我悄悄告诉你，在你们来之前的几天夜里，村里剩下的小巫师、小女巫都投匪军去了！

Sergey: What?
谢尔盖：什么？！

Sergey's Uncle: Tch-tch-tch! They said they'd punish anyone who stands with the cavalry when they return.
姑父：别喊这么大声！他们走之前都说，谁要和骑兵军站一边，等他们杀回来了，就烧谁家的屋子。

He watches the Cavalry Corps soldiers passing by the village entrance.
他忧心忡忡地看着从村口经过的骑兵军。

Sergey's Uncle: If you can't fight them off, they'll burn the village to ashes. So, are you coming with us? Yes or no?
姑父：要是你们打不过，全村都要被他们烧个一干二净了。所以，你到底和不和我逃难去？

Vertin: I see Lilya.
维尔汀：我看到红弩箭了。

A small group of reinforcements makes its slow advance across the grassland. A transport team carrying guns and artillery rumbles close behind.
一支小规模的援军在草原上缓缓而行，运送枪弹与火炮的车队则缀在队伍之后，车轴隆隆作响。

Sonetto: Hmm. That must be the commander of the Zeno cavalry beside her.
十四行诗：她身边的人……应该就是芝诺骑兵军的军长了。

The Cavalry Corps officers awaiting them ride out to the head of the team to guide them to their destination.
在此等候的骑兵军士官骑着马来到援军队列前，引导他们前往指定地点。

Before the commander can reach them, an old friend steps forward to greet the Timekeeper.
老朋友先军长一步迎上司辰。

Lilya: Captain! Why did the Foundation send you here? Isn't exactly the safest place in the world right now.
红弩箭：——头儿！基金会怎么偏偏在这个时候放你过来了？现在可一点也不太平。

Vertin: I applied for it. There's something I need to find in Bereggrad.
维尔汀：是我主动申请的，我需要在别列格勒搜寻一些情报……

Vertin: Don't give me that look, Lilya. Madam Z approved it.
维尔汀：别这么看着我，红弩箭。Z女士批准了我这次外勤申请。

Lilya: Alright, what Z says goes.
红弩箭：好吧，既然是Z女士同意过的。

Sonetto: I'm glad to see you safe and sound, Ms. Lilya. Your message had us worried.
十四行诗：很高兴得知你安然无恙，红弩箭，在收到你的消息时我们都很紧张。

Lilya: Hah. The Foundation seriously needs to update their communication devices. At least, make them bulletproof.
红弩箭：基金会应该更新一下通讯设备了，特殊时期，通讯器也该做成防弹的才对。

The commander approaches as they chat.
军长靠近正在攀谈的队伍。

Commander Ivan: Timekeeper.
军长伊万：司辰。

Vertin: Commander Ivan, it's a pleasure to meet you. If you could, a brief walk-through of the situation in Bereggrad and the surrounding areas would be most helpful.
维尔汀：你好，军长。我希望尽快了解别列格勒及周边的情况。

Commander Ivan: Of course. But tell me, where did that company behind you spring from? I received no word in our communications.
军长伊万：我会向你们说明的。在此之前，我想问问这一支部队是哪儿来的？通讯中没有提到过他们。

The commander's eyes settle on a small detachment within the reinforcements, about a hundred soldiers strong, led by a red-haired knight.
军长看向援军中的一支百人左右的小队伍，他们由一位骑士所率领。

The knight notices him, too. She dismounts and steps forward.
领队的红发骑士也发现了军长正在注意自己，便主动下马，靠上前来。

Marsha: Vice Admiral Burakov, I am the medic of the Foundation's special unit "XII." Behind me are the soldiers returning from the front lines and our field medical team.
玛尔纱：布列科夫少将，我是基金会“Ⅻ”特别行动小队的队医，我身后的是从前线返乡的士兵，还有一支野战医疗团队。

Marsha: Every one of them is willing to enlist in the cavalry and join the fight against the bandits.
玛尔纱：他们愿意加入骑兵军，参加对抗匪军的战斗。

Commander Ivan: Good to know.
军长伊万：……这再好不过。

Commander Ivan: Go to Colonel Sabina Sternova. She will see your men settled.
军长伊万：去找莎宾娜·斯捷尔诺娃上校，她会安排好你带来的这些人。

Marsha: Yes, Commander.
玛尔纱：我明白了。

The knight leads the special unit away.
骑士带领这支特别的队伍离去。

Commander Ivan: Follow me, if you will, Timekeeper.
军长伊万：请随我来吧，司辰。

Indoors
村庄室内

Commander Ivan: Intelligence has spoken: the scattered bandit forces are converging upstream of the Don. They carry Zeno weaponry, though their identity is yet unknown.
军长伊万：……目前，顿河零散的匪军有合流之势，他们得到了来源不明的芝诺武装，集中在相对靠近上游的位置。

Commander Ivan: Most likely, this is Igor's doing, though we've kept clear of them thus far, as headquarters ordered.
军长伊万：有很大概率是伊戈尔主导了这一切，目前为止，按照指挥部的命令，我们没有与对方主力发生过大规模冲突。

Commander Ivan: Villages have been plundered. Many have fled downstream, yet most cling to their homes.
军长伊万：部分上游村庄已经遭到匪军洗劫。少数市民已经向下游逃难了，但是大多数还留守在城中不愿离开。

The commander lifts his eyes from the map, still deep in thought.
军长从地图上抬起眼，露出了若有所思的神情。

Commander Ivan: Zeno has ordered the cavalry station in Bereggrad until your mission is complete. We ride at dawn.
军长伊万：根据芝诺的命令，骑兵军会在明早启程进城防守，直到你完成在别列格勒城内的任务。

Vertin: Many of your cavalrymen are locals from the era. What do they think of Igor?
维尔汀：骑兵军里有很多这个时代的本地人，他们对伊戈尔的认知是怎样的？

Commander Ivan: What happened in São Paulo is classified. To them, he's a warlord like any other.
军长伊万：按照规定，他们没被告知圣保罗的事。伊戈尔在他们眼中是一个普通的军阀领袖。

Commander Ivan: We do have a controlled route to the city, but Timekeeper, are you certain you wish to enter? The rebels could strike at any moment.
军长伊万：虽然我们还控制着一条进出城市的道路，但你要在这种情况下进城吗，司辰？叛军随时可能对城市发动攻击。

Vertin: Yes. I'll complete my mission quickly, hopefully before any conflict arises.
维尔汀：是的。至少城内还没有发生战斗，我需要尽快完成调查任务。

Commander Ivan: I see. Then tell me the details of your mission so that I may lend my hand.
军长伊万：为了能更好地配合你的行动，我能了解任务的具体细节吗？

Vertin: There's a place in the city called the Amber Room. It's said to hold an important clue about the "Storm."
维尔汀：我需要在城内找到一个与“暴雨”有关的重要线索，它与一个叫做“琥珀屋”的地方有关。

Vertin: Does it sound familiar at all, Commander?
维尔汀：这就是我知道的全部。你对这个词有印象吗，军长？

Commander Ivan: Not the Amber Room in St. Petersburg, I presume. We're a long way off from there.
军长伊万：我想你说的不是放在圣彼得堡的那座国家宝藏，不然你也不会到一千公里外的别列格勒来了。

Commander Ivan: Then no, can't say it does. It seems I cannot aid you much in this.
军长伊万：我在别列格勒从未听人提起过它，看来我没法为你提供别的援助。

The commander pauses, then asks the question that's been gnawing at him since the Timekeeper's arrival.
军长顿了顿，问出了自己更加关心的问题。

Commander Ivan: Timekeeper, may I ask: besides your "Storm" investigation, are you also here to deal with the Golden Famine?
军长伊万：司辰，除了调查“暴雨”线索外，你身上是否还肩负调查“金色饥荒”的任务？

Commander Ivan: We tried ourselves, but Zeno soldiers have little experience in arcane matters. We couldn't unearth the root.
军长伊万：我们尝试过自己解决，但芝诺的军人不具备调查神秘学事件的经验，很难找到症结所在。

Vertin: I'm sorry. I didn't receive any information on that matter. But I'll keep an eye out for you.
维尔汀：抱歉，我没有接到相关的指示，但我会尽量留意可能的线索。

Commander Ivan: I see.
军长伊万：……我明白了。

Commander Ivan: The cavalry will aid you in any way we can. But with our strength as it is, I cannot guarantee the safety of Bereggrad.
军长伊万：骑兵军会全力协助你。可是目前我们拥有的兵力，加上援军，也很难保证别列格勒的安全。

Vertin: I'm not sure how much it will help, but I heard reinforcements are on the way.
维尔汀：虽然我不确定这会有多大帮助，但是我在不久前刚得知了新一批即将到来的增援。

Vertin: The newly formed Flying Witch Company will arrive soon, along with a new broomstick for Lilya.
维尔汀：一支新组建的飞行巫师中队马上就会抵达红柳树村，她们还多带来了一把新的飞行扫帚。

Lilya suddenly perks up.
一旁昏昏欲睡的红弩箭来了兴趣。

Lilya: Zeno reformed the Flying Witches?
红弩箭：芝诺重新组建了飞行巫师中队？

Vertin: No, this unit belongs to the Foundation.
维尔汀：不，这是属于基金会的飞行中队。

Vertin: Lilya, Committee member Irinei asks if you'll serve as its temporary captain. What do you say?
维尔汀：另外，红弩箭，伊里内伊委员委托我问你，你愿意在这次行动中临时担任飞行中队的队长吗？

Sternova gestures to a lonely, empty house.
在她面前的是一座孤独的空房子。

Sternova: We haven't seen the owner of this place for two years. The elder said you can stay here for a few days.
斯捷尔诺娃：以前住在这间屋子里的人有两年没有回来了。我问过村长，他同意把这里借给你住几天。

Marsha: This is spacious.
玛尔纱：这里……还挺宽敞的。

If you ignore its decrepit state, it is a fairly decent place for a short stay.
只要能忽视它摇摇欲坠的结构，这还算是一间不错的临时住所。

Sternova: Um, yeah. The stable in the yard still needs some fixing up, so the cavalry will have to take care of your horse, if you don't mind.
斯捷尔诺娃：嗯，就是院子里的马厩已经倒了，一直没有修好。不介意的话，你的马可以交给骑兵军统一照料。

Marsha: That would be kind of you. Thank you very much.
玛尔纱：那再好不过，麻烦你们了。

Sternova: It's no big deal. What's one more horse to feed?
斯捷尔诺娃：没事，骑兵军有的是马，多一匹马也不算什么事。

Sternova hands the key to Marsha and leads her horse away.
斯捷尔诺娃把房屋的钥匙交给玛尔纱之后，牵着她的马离开了。

Just as Marsha is about to slip the key inside the lock, a rustling sound catches her attention.
玛尔纱转过身，正准备将钥匙插入锁孔，便被一阵窸窣声吸引了注意力。

Marsha: Is that a rat?
玛尔纱：有老鼠？

Not the sound a rat would make.
——可这大得不像是老鼠能发出来的声音。

A small black shadow vanishes behind the once-leafy elm in the corner of the yard.
一个小小的黑色阴影消失在院落一旁曾经茂盛过的榆树下。

Marsha: Hm? Or a child from the village?
玛尔纱：嗯？是村里的小孩吗？

Marsha: Never mind. I need to rest.
玛尔纱：算了……先进房屋休息吧。

She hears it again. She looks over to the same spot.
同样的动静再次传来，玛尔纱再次朝可疑的地点投去目光。

Marsha: Who's there?
玛尔纱：到底是谁躲在那儿？

From behind the elm, a head peeks out, briefly meeting her eyes.
在榆树干后，她的目光与一颗探出来的脑袋短暂相遇。

???: ...!
？？？：……！

This time, the shadow doesn't hide itself behind the tree but disappears in the glow of an arcane skill.
这一次，人影不再依靠掩体遮蔽身形，而是消失在一道神秘术的辉光中。

Marsha: Vanished? Just like that?
玛尔纱：消失了？

This could be a threat. Marsha draws her weapon and moves toward the fading arcane light.
玛尔纱警惕起来——她很难再把躲藏者视作无害对象。骑士拿出武器，向着神秘术辉光消散的方向而去。

A shadow flashes past once more.
一抹团状的黑色影子又一次在视线中一闪而过。

Marsha: What? That was definitely a person.
玛尔纱：这是什么东西？刚刚看见的明明是一个人影才对。

Marsha: A person sneaking in the shadows around the cavalry's station. Suspicious to be sure. A spy, perhaps?
玛尔纱：这么躲躲藏藏的实在是太可疑了，而且还是在骑兵军驻扎的村子里……难道是间谍？

*a strange sound*
“沙沙——”

Its direction is exposed again.
声音再次暴露出方位。

Turning, Marsha stares intently.
在追逐之前，玛尔纱凝神审视——

Marsha: Are my eyes playing tricks on me? Is that a fiddler crab? On the Don steppe?
玛尔纱：……我没看错吧，那对蟹螯……是招潮蟹？在顿河草原上？

The knight runs toward the dubious fiddler crab at astonishing speed. Her heavy armor doesn't seem to slow her down in the slightest.
骑士迈开脚步，以惊人的速度向可疑的招潮蟹靠近，沉重的盔甲也完全没有影响她的速度。

In its desperation, her opponent flees sideways toward the outskirts of the village.
而对方几乎是慌不择路地横着向村外逃窜。

But there is no escape. The knight is closing in on the crab.
二人之间的距离越来越近。

Marsha: Got you!
玛尔纱：——抓到你了！

Forced into a corner, the fiddler crab snaps its oversized claws in the air.
被逼到墙角的海栖魔精咔嚓作响地在空中挥舞巨螯。

Fiddler Crab?: Sssss, ssss!
招潮蟹？：嘶嘶……嘶嘶嘶——！

Marsha: Enough, shapeshifter! This is absurd! How would a fiddler crab make it out here to the plain?
玛尔纱：放弃吧，你的变形术也太拙劣了，招潮蟹怎么可能会在草原上出现？

Marsha: And might I remind you that you're too big to be a crab?
玛尔纱：而且作为一只招潮蟹，你的体型也太大了。

Fiddler Crab?: Sss ...
招潮蟹？：嘶……

The same glow returns, the light surrounding the fiddler crab bending and distorting.
辉光再次闪烁着，环绕着招潮蟹周身的光线开始扭曲变形。

It reveals the figure of a girl.
施术者终于放弃了无用的神秘术。

Merel: ...
梅蕾尔：……

The newly appointed medic of the "XII" Squad locks eyes with her team's deserter.
新晋的“Ⅻ”小队队医与逃亡中的“Ⅻ”小队队员四目相对。

Merel: H-Hey, nice to see you again. Heh.
梅蕾尔：又……又见面了——

Marsha: Merel?
玛尔纱：梅，蕾，尔？

Marsha: H-How did you ...
玛尔纱：你怎么……

Marsha steps forward. Caught at last, Merel squats down, covering her head with both hands.
玛尔纱向前一步。
被抓捕的逃兵立刻下蹲，双手护住头顶。

Merel: D-Don't hurt me! I'll tell you everything.
梅蕾尔：别……别打我！我什么都说！

Marsha: You mean to say you plan to stay here until the war's over?
玛尔纱：……你是说，打算就这么混在骑兵军里直到战争结束？

Merel: Well, I don't really have much choice. At least with the cavalry, I won't starve.
梅蕾尔：嗯……现在除了待在骑兵军里，外面都没有东西可以吃了。

Marsha: And if the "Storm" comes? What then?
玛尔纱：如果“暴雨”来了呢？你要怎么办？

Merel: I'll be fine. I took an Equilibrium Umbrella with me. And maybe in the next era, I'll find a safer place to stay.
梅蕾尔：我带了一把平衡伞，说不定到了下一个时代，我可以找到安全的地方……

Her face sinks.
她神色一沉，变得心虚而不安。

Merel: Oh. Oh no. I lost it when the bandits chased me.
梅蕾尔：我、我被土匪追杀的时候把伞弄丢了……

Marsha: ...
玛尔纱：……

Merel: Oourgh!
梅蕾尔：唔！

The knight grabs her face with both hands, pressing her cheeks tight together.
骑士的一双手扣住逃兵的脸，使劲揉搓。

Merel: Let go.
梅蕾尔：补……方开……<不……放开……>

Marsha: This is serious, Merel! Do you have any idea what they do to deserters? Do you think hiding with Zeno will prevent the Foundation from finding you?
玛尔纱：现在是战时，你知道逃兵被抓到会怎么处置吗？你觉得一直混在芝诺的队伍里就不会有基金会的人发现你？

Merel: I ... I don't know.
梅蕾尔：唔——窝……喔不知岛……<唔——我……我不知道……>

Marsha: You may survive one "Storm," but what about the next? And the others after that?
玛尔纱：躲得了一次“暴雨”，躲得了下一次吗？

Merel: Please ... Marsha ...
梅蕾尔：秋……秋妮，玛尔……<求……求你，玛尔……>

Marsha: How will you know which era the "Storm" will take us to next?
玛尔纱：你又怎么知道下一次会到一个怎样的时代？

Merel: Marsha ... Can't ... breathe ...
梅蕾尔：玛尔纱……窝补能——湖西……<玛尔纱……我不能——呼吸……>

She lets go.
玛尔纱骤然松开手。

Marsha: Honestly! What am I to do with you?
玛尔纱：真是要被你急死了。

Merel hangs her head, rubbing her reddened cheeks.
梅蕾尔垂下脑袋，揉着被搓红的脸颊。

Merel: So, why are you here?
梅蕾尔：你为什么会到这里来？

Marsha: I was taking a group of Don Cossacks home and heard the Timekeeper was going the same direction, so we met on the road and traveled the rest of the way together.
玛尔纱：我要带一队偶遇的顿河人回家，正好与司辰顺路，就和她在路上会合，一起过来了。

Marsha: Now that I have arrived, Creius has requested I stay by the Timekeeper's side until she completes her mission. Then I'll return with her.
玛尔纱：现在我的事情完成了。刻雷乌斯要求我在这段时间留在司辰身边，等她的任务完成之后一起回基金会总部。

Merel: Y-You're not going to bring me back to Creius, are you?
梅蕾尔：……你……是不是要抓我回去？见刻雷乌斯？

Marsha leans against a worn pillar, looking deeply downcast.
玛尔纱靠在磨损老旧的屋梁柱上，脸色十分难看。

Marsha: If only I could. But with Creius comes the Foundation. Either way, I'll be sure to bring you back. Running around alone out here is far more dangerous than anything the Foundation could do.
玛尔纱：要是见刻雷乌斯倒还好了，就怕基金会也来插手。但我肯定要带你回去的，在外面逃跑只会更危险。

Merel knows there's nothing left she can do.
逃亡的少女知道自己没有反抗的手段。

Marsha: Does anybody else here know you, aside from me?
玛尔纱：除了我之外，这里有没有人认识你？

Merel: No.
梅蕾尔：没有……

The image of the young Foundation "big shot" appears in Merel's mind. She shakes her head, trying to erase those gray eyes from her memory.
“基金会老爷”年轻的脸出现在梅蕾尔脑子里，她摇摇头，把那双灰色眼睛从记忆中甩出去。

Merel: I know the Timekeeper and Lieutenant Lilya, but I don't think they know me.
梅蕾尔：我认识司辰和红弩箭，但她们应该都不认识我。

Merel: But Sonetto, Vertin's assistant ... I'm not so sure. We were classmates for a few years, but we didn't talk much.
梅蕾尔：只是维尔汀的助手……我说不准，十四行诗和我当过几年同学，虽然我们也没怎么打过交道。

Marsha: I see. Stay close with me, then, and don't let Sonetto see you. It would be best if we solved this without any interference from a third party.
玛尔纱：那你暂时跟在我身边，尽量别让十四行诗看见。如果这事能在队内解决，或许还好说。

Marsha: Paravyan and I may be able to plead your case, but if we're to have any chance of being successful, you can't run away. Not again.
玛尔纱：我和帕拉维扬也许可以给你说情……但这一切的前提是，你不准再逃跑了。

Merel: Urgh!
梅蕾尔：唔——！

A sharp noise breaks the silence, startling the still-nervy Merel. She jerks her head up but sees only a few swirling trails of condensation lingering in the sky.
一道尖锐的破空声打破了好不容易平复下来的情绪，少女猛地抬头，却只看见空中留下的几道蜿蜒气流。

Merel: What is that?
梅蕾尔：天上是什么？

Marsha looks up too, but she shows no sign of alarm.
玛尔纱也抬头望向天空，但并未有任何慌张。

Marsha: It's the Foundation's flight unit. They're here.
玛尔纱：是基金会的飞行中队，她们到了。

11  笨蛋还是天才？ / Fool or Genius?

SPDM Library Card
第一防线学校图书馆借阅卡

Fiddler Crab Shapeshifting for Dummies
—Call Number: F-4312-A
—Category: Shapeshifting - Marine Critter Biology Branch

Borrowing Record
Sonetto: Returned after 2 hours and 36 minutes.
Hans Levis: Returned after one week.
...
Cyril Aman: Returned the following day at the same time (abandoned practice, claiming to hear the sound of tsunamis in their nightmares).
Lark von Stuhr: Returned after 5 days and 6 hours.
Merel: Returned after 2 days and 3 hours.

*Librarian's Note:
Aside from Sonetto and Merel, all returns showed claw marks along the spine and traces of salt residue.
Going forward, this book may only be borrowed under teacher supervision.
《笨蛋也能学会的招潮蟹变形术》
——馆藏编号：F-4312-A
——类别：形体炼成·海洋魔精生物学分支

借阅记录
十四行诗 两小时零三十六分归还
汉斯·李维斯 一周后归还
……
西里尔·阿曼 次日同一时间归还（声称噩梦中出现海啸声，放弃练习。）
拉克·冯·斯图尔 五天零六小时
梅蕾尔 两天零三小时

*图书管理员批注：
除十四行诗与梅蕾尔外，其余同学归还时，书脊留有钳状痕迹与少量盐屑。

12  一个儿子的自白 / A Son's Confession

A Son's Confession
一个儿子的自白

Lilya: Faster!
红弩箭：——再快一点！

Lilya speeds low across the plain, the newest flying broomstick model cutting a narrow V shape through the grass.
红弩箭急速掠过低空，新型飞行扫帚在草场上劈开狭长的“V”形波浪纹。

Alpha Two: Lieutenant! We're losing sight of you!
阿尔法2号：我们快看不见你了，中尉！

Lilya: Then you better learn how to get your speed up to 240 in five seconds.
红弩箭：如果你们想跟上我的话，就快点学会怎么在5秒内把速度推到240。

A series of vague replies come through the communicator, their voices strained as they struggle to catch up to their captain.
队内通讯中传出含混不清的应答声。这些声音信号哀号着，苦苦追上了飞在最前端的中队长。

Alpha Two: But at this height, according to the manual—
阿尔法2号：可是在这个高度，按照飞行扫帚操作手册——

Lilya: The manual? Pft, that's for nerds! Are you nerds?
红弩箭：什么？操作手册？——那是写给书呆子看的，现在我是你们的队长。

Lilya: Move into split formation and prepare to climb! Brooms up!
红弩箭：按照预定队形分出两队，准备爬升，记得给我把杆拉到底！

Alpha Two: C-Copy that!
阿尔法2号：明——明白！

Twenty-four Flying Witches follow their leader into the clouds above, slicing the sky in two with their vertical trails.
二十四名飞行巫师追随领头的隼儿直冲云霄，拖曳出垂直于地面的尾流，将身后的天幕切成两半。

Lilya: Hold altitude. See the targets by the river?
红弩箭：够了，保持现在的高度，现在你们能看见河边的那些靶子了吗？

Alpha Two: Yes, Lieutenant.
阿尔法2号：能看得见，中尉。

Lilya: Good. Real enemies won't just stand around and wait for you to strike, but training like this can sharpen your eye.
红弩箭：很好，虽然真正的敌人不会停在原地挨打，但必须要先拿这些无聊的东西练练手。

Lilya: Flank the target from both sides, avoid friendly fire, and dive!
红弩箭：以相对方向夹击目标，注意避开友军……现在——俯冲向下！

*boom*
“轰——”

After a successful firing dive, the two flight teams effortlessly reunite in the sky. They rendezvous, hover, and merge into a single formation.
一轮成功的俯冲扫射之后，两支飞行队姿态轻巧地在空中交会、盘旋，又重新合为一队。

Commander Ivan: ...
军长伊万：……

The howl of engines quickly drowns out the commander's conversation as the flight unit descends onto the open ground outside Tamarovka.
谈话声被逐渐嘈杂的引擎呼啸所掩盖，飞行中队已抵达红柳树村前的开阔地，正渐次降落。

With her feet firm on the ground, Lilya passes her broom to her second-in-command.
领队的女巫降落妥当，将扫帚交给队副。

Lilya: Captain. Oh, Commander.
红弩箭：头儿，军长——

The swirling air around her sends Lilya's pristine uniform fluttering like a flag in the wind.
飞行女巫穿越环绕周身的混乱气浪，崭新的制服如旗帜般猎猎作响。

She walks straight toward the cavalry and Foundation leaders, who stand at the entrance of the village, observing the training.
她径直走向在村口观摩训练的骑兵军将领与基金会要人。

Lilya: New broom's nice. Rookies aren't too bad either. Only problem is, they rely on the tech too much. Should spend a little more time on their arcane skills.
红弩箭：基金会新扫帚还不赖，这些新兵也是，最大的问题是她们太信赖机械，对神秘术的掌握还欠些火候。

Lilya: Have to say, I never thought I'd wear a Foundation uniform. Wait. Don't tell me I have to follow the Committee's orders from now on.
红弩箭：还有，我从没想过有一天会穿上基金会的制服……等等，我想这不意味着我之后要听委员会的话了吧？

Vertin: Don't worry. You're still on Team Timekeeper. Your new uniform's just for easy identification while you're acting as flight captain.
维尔汀：你的编制仍然留在司辰小队中，红弩箭。这身衣服是为了方便你履行飞行中队长的职责。

Lilya: Alright.
红弩箭：这还不错。

Commander Ivan: You have some time to sharpen your little birds, but conflict rides fast. Work quick.
军长伊万：在可能的战斗爆发之前，你还有一些时间训练你的队员，需要尽快与她们磨合。

Commander Ivan: The Foundation swore us enough fuel and ammunition for your entire unit and more besides.
军长伊万：基金会承诺会为我们提供更多军需品，包括供应整个飞行中队的燃料和弹药。

Commander Ivan: You alone have access to it. No applications, no paperwork, just go directly to the quartermaster.
军长伊万：这些东西只对你们有用，你可以直接从军需官处获取飞行中队需要的物资，不用再打申请。

Lilya: Nice. I'll make sure the rookies never step foot on the ground.
红弩箭：真棒，我一定会让这些新兵脚不沾地的。

Vertin: Mr. Irinei said that this is the Su-02ве's combat debut. He hopes to hear some feedback from you.
维尔汀：伊里内伊委员告诉我，这将是Su-02ве型飞行扫帚第一次投入实战，他希望你能给他一些使用反馈。

Lilya: Well, it's faster, got stronger firepower and better maneuverability. Basically an all-round improvement.
红弩箭：速度更快，机动性更好，火力更凶猛……差不多就是这些，算是全方位的提升。

Lilya: Haven't used it for long, though. Any major defects you know of?
红弩箭：相比之下，我更关心这种试验机有没有什么重大缺陷。

Vertin: The price.
维尔汀：它们非常贵。

Vertin: One 02 costs nearly triple the amount of an 01, or even more.
维尔汀：02型的制造成本是01型的三倍以上。

Lilya: Who's paying for repairs?
红弩箭：那如果我摔坏了它，这账要算到谁的头上？

Vertin: Me. The Su-02ве assigned to you is the property of Team Timekeeper.
维尔汀：配发给你的Su-02ве被算在司辰小队的编制下。

Lilya: Okay. I'll do my best not to break it.
红弩箭：……行吧，我会好好呵护它的。

Noon descends. The sun hangs tilted in the sky, casting an unseasonable dryness across the grassland.
时间已到正午，斜挂晴空的太阳在草原上投下不属于这个季节的燥意。

The aroma of simple meals drifts from the households. Whether in times of famine or prosperity, the plain scent of wheat is equally tempting.
各家窗中都飘出了粗制饭食的香气，无论是饥荒还是富足的年代，朴素的麦香都同样诱人。

Commander Ivan: That is all. Dismissed.
军长伊万：那就这样吧，解散。

With the order given, the high-ranking officials outside the village drift away. Lilya turns to glance at the unit still holding position.
命令下达，围绕在村口的高级将领纷纷散去。红弩箭回头看了一眼还在原地待命的飞行中队队员。

Lilya: Captain, I need to go take care of the baby birds.
红弩箭：头儿，我得去照顾这些雏鸟了。

Vertin: Alright. See you later, Lilya.
维尔汀：嗯，稍后再见，红弩箭。

The temporary captain of the flight unit turns away.
飞行中队临时队长转身离去。

Commander Ivan: Timekeeper.
军长伊万：……司辰。

Vertin: Anything I can help you with, Commander?
维尔汀：有什么事吗，军长？

Commander Ivan: Hungry? Come to my home for a meal, won't you? I'll send for your assistant too.
军长伊万：如果你们还没有准备午饭的话，不如去我家吃一顿便饭吧，我会派人去叫你的助手。

Commander Ivan: My mother's stew could raise a fallen horse. She would love for you to try it.
军长伊万：我母亲非常希望你们能尝尝她做的炖菜。

A simple invitation. Yet under the circumstances, its recipient perceives a subtle unease.
这是一句简单的邀请，但在眼下的境况中，听者却察觉出一丝异样。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Vertin: Sorry, I was just thinking about something. Do you mean to say that your home is in Tamarovka?
维尔汀：抱歉，我刚有点走神。你是在说，你家，还有你的母亲，也在红柳树村吗？

Commander Ivan: That's right. Will you come?
军长伊万：是的，你意下如何？

Indoors
村庄室内

The commander pushes open an old wooden door, followed by two special guests.
军长推开了一扇老旧的木门，他身后还跟着两位特别的访客。

Faint candlelight reveals a rope strung above their heads where dried vegetables and smoked meat should be hung, but now hangs bare.
昏暗的烛光映照着悬挂在头顶的绳索——那里空荡荡的，本来应该系着今年过冬用的干菜和熏肉。

Commander Ivan: Mama.
军长伊万：妈妈。

Galina: Vanyusha? Did you bring guests?
佳琳娜夫人：万纽沙——你带客人来了吗？

Vertin: Good afternoon, madam. I'm Vertin, Timekeeper of the Foundation. And this is Sonetto, my assistant.
维尔汀：你好，夫人，我是基金会的司辰，维尔汀。这一位是我的助手，十四行诗。

Galina: Oh! So you're the one—
佳琳娜夫人：哎呀，您就是——

The elderly lady fails to hide her surprise.
老妇人难掩惊异之色。

Galina: When Vanyusha told me there would be important guests joining us, I hardly expected them to be so young.
佳琳娜夫人：万纽沙告诉我，今天会和基金会来的大人物见面，我从没想到您会是这个年纪。

Galina: Oh, I mean nothing by it, dear. Just the words of an old woman. Honestly, Vanyusha, you must tell me if you plan to bring growing young ladies to lunch.
佳琳娜夫人：不不，我没别的意思……还是别在意我了。万纽沙，为什么你不告诉我她还是个孩子？

Ms. Galina gives a sheepish look at the cabbage soup, hard bread, and smoked fish before her.
佳琳娜夫人看着眼前的一锅菜汤，以及一些干硬的面包和熏鱼，面露愁容。

Commander Ivan: We're just here for a simple meal, Mama. No feasts while others starve.
军长伊万：我们就吃平常的东西，妈妈。这个时节不要再浪费食物了。

Sonetto: The meal looks delicious, madam. More than enough for us.
十四行诗：感谢您的招待，夫人，这些东西对我们来说足够了。

Ivan pulls out his mother's chair and helps her to her seat.
伊万走到母亲身边，扶她坐下。

Commander Ivan: Timekeeper, Ms. Sonetto, sit, eat.
军长伊万：司辰，十四行诗小姐，把这里当自己家，不用太拘谨。

Vertin: Thank you.
维尔汀：谢谢。

Galina: You will have to forgive me. This is all I have to offer at the moment.
佳琳娜夫人：只能叫你这么将就地吃一顿了……

Ivan takes a piece of rock-hard bread and softens it in the hot soup. His young guests follow suit.
伊万拿起一块硬如岩石的面包，在热汤里泡软。客人也学着他的方式用餐。

Commander Ivan: You have food enough for the winter, Mama?
军长伊万：过冬的粮食还够吗？

Galina: Oh, don't worry about me. Though, if things go on like this, who can say?
佳琳娜夫人：我一个人吃是够了，不用担心我。但是不长麦子的秋天会持续多久，谁也没个准数。

Galina: Ms. Timekeeper, are you here to end the famine?
佳琳娜夫人：司辰老爷，您是来帮我们看看这怪天气的吗？

Vertin: Please, call me Vertin, madam.
维尔汀：请您叫我维尔汀就好，佳琳娜夫人。

Vertin: As for this Golden Famine, I will try my best to give it a thorough investigation.
维尔汀：至于“金色饥荒”……我会尽量弄清楚这件事的起因。

Caught between frustration and hope, the old lady lets out a sigh.
老妇人发出一声叹息，失落与希望的情绪都混在其中。

Galina: You should know, Vanyusha, many have told me to leave with them. But I have stayed, trusting that you will find a way.
佳琳娜夫人：其他邻居劝我一起走，但我想你肯定有办法的，万纽沙，就想留下来等你的消息。

Galina: Some of those fleeing south passed through the village. They said the attackers are different. They only take kalduny—arcanists—as captives.
佳琳娜夫人：有很多人从上游逃难过来，经过我们村子，都说从没见过那样的土匪，他们只要神秘学家，不要人类。

Vertin: Arcanists?
维尔汀：神秘学家？

Galina: Yes. The poor things who refused to cooperate were beaten half to death before they took them. As for the humans, well ...
佳琳娜夫人：是的，只要神秘学家，不配合的就会被打个半死，然后拖走。人类……

She simply shakes her head.
她摇摇头，不愿再说下去。

Sonetto: But Igor's army would've taken humans too.
十四行诗：如果是伊戈尔的军队，不应该只接受神秘学家……

Galina: Igor?
佳琳娜夫人：伊戈尔？

Vertin: Sonetto, you must try this pickled watermelon.
维尔汀：十四行诗，尝尝这块腌西瓜。

A spoonful of sour, dark-red flesh is suddenly shoved into the chief assistant's mouth, stopping her from revealing any more.
一勺泛着酸味、色泽暗红的果肉突兀地堵在第一助手嘴边，打断了她的自言自语。

Sonetto: Hm? O-Okay.
十四行诗：唔？……好，好的。

Vertin: It really is delicious, madam. Quite different from any I've ever tasted. Might I ask how you made it?
维尔汀：您做的腌菜非常美味，夫人，我从没在其他地方吃过这种东西，它是怎么做出来的？

Galina: Ah, it warms an old woman's heart to hear such things. Nothing special, really. Though many underestimate the importance of the barrel.
佳琳娜夫人：啊，你们喜欢吃就再好不过。其实每家每户的腌西瓜都差不多，只是很多人都没注意，腌桶也很重要……

As Ms. Galina steadily shares her years-tested method of pickling watermelon, they leave behind talk of these troubled times.
佳琳娜夫人细致耐心地为客人们讲解腌西瓜的技巧，在座的人则再也没有谈论过时局，只是闷头吃午餐。

The sounds of wooden spoons scraping against steel bowls mingle with chewing and swallowing.
木勺与铁质碗盆刮擦出凌乱的声响，其间夹杂着咀嚼吞咽食物的声音。

...
……

The lunch finally ends in an uneasy silence. Leaning against the door, Ms. Galina says goodbye to her guests.
午餐最终在有些生涩的沉默中结束，佳琳娜夫人倚在门框上为客人送别。

Galina: Apologies again for the poor meal.
佳琳娜夫人：……实在是我没有招待好。

Commander Ivan: Hush now. The meal was hearty, and our bellies are full. There are few stews better than yours.
军长伊万：不要这么想，妈妈，我们吃得都很好。我会一直惦记你做的炖菜的。

Vertin: Yes, it was just what we needed, madam.
维尔汀：我也一样，夫人。

After her son's reassuring words, the old lady finally closes the door.
在军长的劝慰下，老妇人终于关上了屋门。

Commander Ivan: I know you have questions, but this is not the place to talk.
军长伊万：我知道你有很多想问的，但这里不是说话的地方。

Commander Ivan: Come. Walk with me.
军长伊万：请跟我来。

Outskirts, Tamarovka
红柳树村·外围

Outside, layers of golden grass lap at the edge of the village like waves on a beach.
村庄外围，繁茂的金色草原像围拢来的巨浪，一层层拍打着村落的堤岸。

Vertin: How could Ms. Galina be your mother?
维尔汀：——佳琳娜夫人怎么会是你的“母亲”？

The directness of this question adds a touch of chill to the unusually warm air. The commander smooths a wrinkle in his collar.
冷静而直接的提问让奇异的暖风都覆上了一层凉意。军长用手抚平领口的褶皱。

Commander Ivan: Timekeeper, you know the same pain, yes? To witness the "Storm" take away those you love. We all do.
军长伊万：我相信你和我一样，都见过“暴雨”将我们熟悉的，甚至是挚爱的人从面前带走。我们都经历过失去的痛苦。

Commander Ivan: If that face appeared again, full of light and life before your eyes, could you still your heart?
军长伊万：但你能肯定，当记忆中的那张脸鲜活地重现于眼前时，自己真的还能控制住那汹涌的依恋吗？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Commander Ivan: When I saw her again, she was no longer my mother, but a teacher with no children, devoted wholly to her students. She never once left home.
军长伊万：我与她重逢的那个时代，她不是我的“母亲”，而是一位一生无子的教师。她把人生倾注在教育身上，一辈子都没有离开过工作的城市。

Vertin: The reason for this phenomenon still remains unknown.
维尔汀：我们至今不知道这种现象为什么会发生。

Vertin: However, observations have shown that each of them has a complete memory, identity, and social relations. Their independent will is undeniable. We can't force them to be the person we think they are.
维尔汀：可“暴雨”后的他们，有完整的记忆、身份、社会关系——我们不能否认他们的独立意志，把我们的认知强加于他们身上。

Commander Ivan: I know, I know. To speak of the "Storm" would only break her. She would shun me. But in time, she took me as her "child."
军长伊万：我知道，我当然知道。对未经历过“暴雨”的人而言，这种想法太荒谬了……我花了很长的时间，才让佳琳娜夫人接受我这个“孩子”。

Commander Ivan: In the end, I have a mother again. That is enough for me.
军长伊万：我重新得到了母亲，这就够了。

Vertin: I'm glad you figured it out.
维尔汀：我很高兴你能想通这一点。

Commander Ivan: Figured it out? Heh, the truth is the opposite, Timekeeper.
军长伊万：想通？不，司辰，我恰恰没有想通。

Commander Ivan: I chased Galina like a lost pup, hoping to find my mother in her.
军长伊万：如果我“想通”了，我就不会追着佳琳娜夫人，希望在她身上寻找我母亲的蛛丝马迹。

Commander Ivan: And I did.
军长伊万：可我真的找到了——

Commander Ivan: Her face, her body, every line and wrinkle—she is the mother I lost to the first "Storm," down to the gentle smile when she talks to me.
军长伊万：五官、皱纹、身形……或者说她所有外在的一切，都与我在第一次“暴雨”中失去的母亲如出一辙。甚至同我说话时，嘴角的弧度都与以往一模一样。

The commander takes several deep breaths to stop his teeth from chattering.
军长深呼吸了几下，好让自己的牙根不再颤抖。

Commander Ivan: Think of it. Those you lost, standing before you again, only their clothes and memory changed.
军长伊万：回想你曾经失去的那些人，当他们现在就出现在你眼前，一切都是那么熟悉……只不过换了一身新的衣服，失去了一些记忆。

Commander Ivan: Would you call them dead and gone? Would you not hold them close, praying memory returns?
军长伊万：你真的不会希望重新回到他们身边吗？你会如此残酷地把那些人从“暴雨”中消失这件事视作死亡吗？

Vertin: It's hard to say.
维尔汀：……我不确定。

Vertin: I've never personally dealt with such a thing. Perhaps I'll have an answer if I do.
维尔汀：我现在还没遇到过这样的事，也许我要经历过之后才知道该如何回答你。

The commander stops his questioning and turns to the meadow bowing before him, withering amid abundance, stretching endlessly into the horizon.
军长不再追问，他转向眼前在风中低俯的草甸——一片在繁茂中枯萎的金黄色，永无止境地绵延到大陆的尽头。

Commander Ivan: It was on land like this that my father first set me on a chestnut mare and showed me the reins.
军长伊万：在这样的草原上，父亲将十岁不到的我抱上一匹栗色的牝马，教我如何控制缰绳。

Commander Ivan: My mother watched on—afraid I would fall, no doubt. But she knew this was the way for every child.
军长伊万：母亲就站在不远处，满脸忧虑，生怕我从马上坠下，但她也知道，这是每一个孩子的必经之路。

Commander Ivan: For centuries, the Cossacks have lived by the steppe. She feeds us, she fights us. Rebellion runs free in our blood.
军长伊万：几个世纪以来，哥萨克都过着这样的生活，在草原中成长，也狂妄地与它交战，如同最叛逆的孩童。

Commander Ivan: The steppe never changes, nor do her people. Sometimes, I find myself asking: Is this truly a false 1920?
军长伊万：草原从未变过，在这里生活的人也一样。有时我确实会恍惚——这真的是一个虚假的1920年吗？

Commander Ivan: You, Timekeeper, you have lived through many eras. We will never wholly understand one another.
军长伊万：司辰，你是一个游走在很多个时代的人。我们之间有区别，不能互相理解。

Vertin: From what I do understand, you are a survivor of the first "Storm." You've lived through all these eras too. I don't see the difference.
维尔汀：据我所知，你从第一次“暴雨”中幸存，之后你也经历过不同的时代，我们没什么不同。

Commander Ivan: True. Yet I am marked by my hometown—my own time long before the "Storm" ever struck.
军长伊万：在经历第一次“暴雨”之前，我的生命就已经被烙下了我自己的时代与故乡的痕迹。

Commander Ivan: I was forged a Cossack, and a Cossack I remain, no matter the era.
军长伊万：它们随着我来到一个又一个新时代，但我永远都只会是一个哥萨克。

Commander Ivan: And you, Timekeeper? Which era is your home? Even 1999 must feel like another world to you.
军长伊万：而你呢，司辰？你自己能说清楚，哪一个时代是属于你的时代吗？就算是1999年，对于你来说就不陌生吗？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Commander Ivan: I do not belittle your path. I only say, losing time and home leaves a wound in the heart.
军长伊万：我绝对不是在否定你生活的意义，我知道失去时代与故国的感觉会在人的胸口上留下多大的创口。

Commander Ivan: Politics aside, I respect you. You are the Timekeeper, the master of all ages and all people. Many consider you a savior.
军长伊万：其实，抛开一切政治因素，我相当尊敬你。你是所有人、所有时代的司辰，还有人把你看作是救世主。

Commander Ivan: And I? I am but a man with mortal ties. Zeno has every reason to keep me at arm's length.
军长伊万：而我只是一个凡人，有自己的家乡和亲族……芝诺不信任我是有理由的。

This is all the old Cossack has to say. He adjusts his wind-blown hat and makes to leave.
老哥萨克觉得自己已经把话说完，便扶正了被风吹斜的帽子，将欲离去。

Vertin: Commander, wait.
维尔汀：等等，军长。

Commander Ivan: Yes? What more is there to say?
军长伊万：我们还有什么话没有说完吗？

Vertin: I have no power to change your decision, but I have a mission here.
维尔汀：我没有权力影响你的决定，但我自己也有来到这里的任务。

Vertin: I need to know. Will you follow Zeno's orders and lead your army to Bereggrad?
维尔汀：我需要知道的是，你真的会按照芝诺的命令，将军队开往别列格勒吗？

Commander Ivan: I will see you safe to the city and stand with you in your mission. This I swear.
军长伊万：我会把你安全送到城内，协助你完成基金会的任务。这是我对你的承诺。

Vertin: I understand.
维尔汀：我明白你的意思了。

???: Commander!
？？？：军长——！

They turn back to see a figure rushing toward them, arms flailing.
站在村外的人回头望去，隔得老远就能望见那道四肢并用的人影。

Sergey: Commander, what are you doing out here? I thought you were at home.
谢尔盖：军长，您怎么到这里来了？刚才我去你家没见到您。

Commander Ivan: What's the matter?
军长伊万：发生了什么事？

Sergey: Th-They got too drunk. A brawl's broken out!
谢尔盖：他们——他们喝了太多，全都打起架来了！

13  特殊时期 / Special Times

Ms. Galina's Wartime Recipes
佳琳娜夫人的战时菜谱

Rye Bread
Aged flour, wheat husks, potato skins, a little salt.
Dry and grind the potato skins, then mix them with the wheat husks into the aged flour. Add warm water and knead it into a dough, then bake beside the stove ashes.
The Golden Famine leaves us short on oil and grain, so there's no oil for the bread. The baked loaf comes out hard and bitter, but at least it fills the stomach. Ivan loves smearing chilli paste on top—he says he's eaten it this way since childhood! I've made a note to prepare extra for him next time.

Smoked Fish
River carp, elm twigs, one old newspaper.
Clean the fish, salt it, then smoke it with elm twigs and newspaper until the skin turns black and dry.
The smell carries three houses down, but don't be greedy. Save the fish oil—freeze it for soup.

Grass Roots Soup
Ingredients: Wild grass roots, nettles, half a potato, a pinch of chili.
Instructions: Boil the roots until soft, add chili, stove ash, and salted grass seeds to mimic borscht. The recipe came from a young man I met by the riverside wheat field. He claimed to be a nameless wandering poet.
*Missing Ingredients: Sour cream, carrots, and onions. Can't make proper borscht. Grass roots have been dug out nearly everywhere. I can only forage along the ditches now.

Jam from Neighbor Valia
Note: They hid two jars of real apple jam before the famine hit, and gave me one ... I've tucked it on the top shelf of the cupboard, wrapped in old cloth so the rats won't smell it.
Her husband lost an entire wheat field's harvest ... May the Mother Earth bless this kind couple.

*I've told Ivan to stop bringing food home. The people at the front have it harder than we do.
Besides, in the face of disaster, soldiers and civilians must be treated equally! Even with his position, I won't allow our family any special treatment.
黑面包
陈面、麦壳、马铃薯皮、少量盐。
将马铃薯皮晾干磨碎，与麦壳混入陈面中，加温水揉成团，放炉灰边烤。
“金色饥荒”导致油粮短缺，不能放油，烤出的黑面包又硬又苦，所幸还能管饱。伊万可喜欢在上面抹辣椒糊，他说他从小就喜欢这么吃！我记下了，下次给他多备点儿。

熏鱼
河鲫、榆树枝、旧报纸一张。
将鱼洗净，撒盐，用榆枝和报纸烟熏，直到鱼皮发黑发干。
香气能飘出三户人家远，但别贪嘴。鱼油要冻起来，留着拌汤用。

草根汤
材料：野草根、荨麻、土豆半只、辣椒一撮。
做法：草根煮烂，加一点辣椒、炉灰、盐渍草籽，模仿红菜汤的味道。配方来自一位在河畔麦田里偶遇的青年，自称无名的流浪诗人。
*缺少材料：酸奶油、胡萝卜与洋葱，没法做红菜汤。最近草根也都被挖得差不多了，只能从沟边采。

邻居瓦莉娅夫人送的果酱
备注：他们家在饥荒前藏了两罐真正的苹果酱，赠送了我一瓶……我把它放进柜子最上层，用旧布包着，不让老鼠闻到。
她的丈夫明明失去了一整片麦田的收成……谢天谢地，愿大地之母保佑这对善良的夫妇。

*我让伊万不要再带粮食回来，前线的人比我们更苦更累。
何况，天灾面前，军民须一视同仁！即使他身居要位，我也绝不同意我们家搞特殊。

14  河流两岸 / Both Sides of the River

Both Sides of the River
河流两岸

Several young men in Zeno cavalry uniforms surround the house, along with some boys not yet old enough to serve.
众多身穿芝诺骑兵制服的青年将房屋包围起来，与之为伴的还有很多年龄更小、未到服役年龄的少年。

A boy and a girl are trapped at the doorstep. Judging by their resemblance, they're siblings. The brother is covered in blood.
一对身穿衬衫的男女被堵在房门口的台阶上。从面貌上来看，这应该是对兄妹。兄长身上已经遍布血污。

Zeno Cavalryman I: Oourgh!
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：唔！

A cavalryman, poised to charge, stumbles back and crashes into the line of men behind him.
准备冲上前的骑兵趔趄着退后，撞在人墙上。

A knight stands at the circle's center, the only barrier between the cavalrymen and the two children.
一位骑士站在包围圈中，成为骑兵与台阶上的兄妹之间唯一的屏障。

Marsha: Who's next?
玛尔纱：还有谁想当下一个？

Zeno Cavalryman II: This has nothing to do with you, outsider!
芝诺骑兵Ⅱ：该死的外乡人，这不关你的事！

Another cavalryman steps out of the crowd, ready to fight.
又一位骑兵从人群中走出，摩拳擦掌。

Commander Ivan: Scatter!
军长伊万：所有人，散开！

One word from the commander immediately silences the cavalrymen.
军长的呵斥立竿见影，喧闹的骑兵们噤若寒蝉。

They instinctively step aside, clearing a path to the house for him.
他们不自觉地退到一旁，为军长让出一条通往房屋的道路。

Marsha: Commander.
玛尔纱：……军长。

The knight puts away her weapon.
骑士和气地收起武器。

Marsha: Forgive me for bruising your soldiers.
玛尔纱：希望你不介意我给你的士兵留下了一些淤青。

Marsha: I don't mean to interfere with the Cavalry Corps's internal affairs, but this incident could cause serious harm to people outside the military.
玛尔纱：我不是在干涉骑兵军的内部事务，只是，这起事件很可能会给军队之外的人造成十分严重的伤害。

The commander raises a hand, prompting her to skip the formalities.
军长以手势告诉玛尔纱，无须在意这场斗殴中的形式细节。

Commander Ivan: What is this?
军长伊万：这里发生了什么？

Marsha: Someone discovered a stash of unfamiliar weapons in these two children's house. It seems their uncle joined the bandits last night.
玛尔纱：有人在这对兄妹家中发现了藏起来的陌生武器。营里的人打探了一番，得知他们的叔叔昨夜出村投奔了匪军。

Commander Ivan: These children. They're from an arcanist family.
军长伊万：这一家人……我记得都是神秘学家。

Marsha: Yes. Some arcanist villagers fled upstream, claiming they would join the bandits and return to rob the others.
玛尔纱：是。之前村里还有少数神秘学家逃向上游，扬言要和匪军一起打回来，抢走其他人的财物。

Marsha: Some soldiers thought it prudent to do a preemptive strike.
玛尔纱：所以，一些人决定提前烧了他们的房子作为报复。

Commander Ivan: ...
军长伊万：……

The commander looks over his soldiers sternly. Their evasive eyes prove the knight's words.
军长以严厉的目光扫视骑兵军士兵，他们闪躲的眼神印证了骑士的说辞确凿无误。

Commander Ivan: Are you hurt?
军长伊万：你有没有受伤？

Marsha: No. They didn't harm me.
玛尔纱：他们没有伤到我。

Commander Ivan: You've stopped the worst. Thank you, Ms. Marsha. Leave them to me.
军长伊万：感谢你没有让事情变得无法挽回，玛尔纱女士，之后的事情交给我处理。

Marsha: Certainly.
玛尔纱：当然。

The knight has no intention to fight. She packs her equipment and takes the cleared path to leave.
骑士无意参与争斗。她收拾好装备，通过骑兵们让出来的一条道路离开了现场。

The commander approaches the young boy on the step. He appears to be below the enlistment age.
军长来到护住妹妹的兄长身边。他看起来还不到参军的年龄。

The commander tries to say something, but sees only refusal in the young boy's eyes. He gives a gentle gesture for them to enter their home.
军长想开口说些什么，但是已经从对方的眼神中看出了抗拒。他只好先放他们回到屋内。

He turns, still standing tall at the doorstep, and faces his men.
他转过身，踏上台阶。

Commander Ivan: There may come a day when we cross blades with our townsfolk—a day none of us prays to see.
军长伊万：也许有一刻，我们会在敌阵中见到自己的同乡——我知道谁都不想打一场这样的仗。

Commander Ivan: This war will scar us. It will scar our children, our children's children. The Don will carry our deeds for centuries.
军长伊万：这场战争会在我们和我们后代的心中留下伤疤。在我们眼前流过的顿河会记住这些事，百年之后也不会遗忘。

Commander Ivan: Those who take up arms with bandits are our enemies. And we will answer with bullet and blade.
军长伊万：投奔匪军的人会成为我们的敌人，我们的子弹与马刀会剖开他们的胸膛。

Commander Ivan: But their families will not suffer. They are our people, her people. No harm will come to them by our hand, else we become those we fight.
军长伊万：但他们的家人不应该遭到报复，我们不能残害自己的同胞，否则我们就和那些土匪没什么两样。

Zeno Cavalryman I: Then-Then what are we waiting for? Why are we stationing in the city?
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：那我们还在等什么？——为什么要进城去？

Zeno Cavalryman II: We aren't city guards. We're riders! We should be out on the steppe hunting down the bandits!
芝诺骑兵Ⅱ：我们不是守城的步兵，应该在城外打败匪军！

Zeno Cavalryman I: R-Right. These are our homes they're destroying. How can we just stay in the city and watch them burn?
芝诺骑兵Ⅰ：没……没错！不能让土匪在城外烧我们的村子。我家就在上游，怎么能让我蹲在城里看着自己的家被烧掉呢？

Zeno Cavalryman II: If the cavalry won't go, then we should go ourselves. Split up by village and defend our own!
芝诺骑兵Ⅱ：如果骑兵军不去，就让我们自己去，每个连的人都回到自己的村子，自己负责！

Commander Ivan: Then the enemy will seize the chance, and one by one, the blood of you and your families will water the Don.
军长伊万：那你们就会一个连接一个连地被敌人杀光，顿河的水会变成你们亲人流出的血！

Zeno Cavalryman: ...
芝诺骑兵：……

Commander Ivan: We fight for the peace of the steppe. If she is to flourish as she once did, then we must stand united upon her plains.
军长伊万：我们只有组成一支团结有纪律的军队，才有资格说，自己是为顿河的和平而战，才能赶走蹂躏土地的施暴者。

Commander Ivan: No more words on this. I forbid it. All those who brawled will be punished as necessary.
军长伊万：我禁止骑兵军中再次发生和讨论这样的事。而今天在场所有参与斗殴的人都将受到应有的处罚。

Commander Ivan: Now, go to your chief and get packed. The rest period is canceled. We ride in one hour.
军长伊万：现在，回去找你们的长官。进城前的休整取消，一小时之后，全体出发。

An hour later, the cavalry leaves Tamarovka, where they have barely paused, to make their advance upstream along the river toward Bereggrad.
一小时后，停留不多时的骑兵军离开了红柳树村，沿着河流向上游的别列格勒城而去。

Thousands of cavalrymen make up most of the head of the marching column. Behind them follow a small number of infantry units and a long supply team.
上万名骑兵占据了行军队列头部的主要长度，在这之后的是少量的步兵，以及漫长的辎重队伍。

Sergey: Ah, so good you joined me at the vanguard. Interesting company, the two of you, what with all your stories from the west!
谢尔盖：你们能同意和我一起走前锋真是太好了，和你们聊天有意思得很，可以听听外面的故事。

Merel: Pretty sure it was you who insisted on coming with us.
梅蕾尔：不是你非要和我们一起走的吗……

Marsha: Heh. Reminds me of my days scouting for the Order. It wasn't an easy position to be in.
玛尔纱：这倒让我想起在骑士团的时候，我也当过好几次斥候骑兵。不过这可不是一个能开小差的职位。

Sergey looks up at the sky.
谢尔盖仰头看了看天。

Sergey: Hm. At this rate, it'll be dark before half the Corps sees the city. Best we hop off and wait for the others to catch up.
谢尔盖：这样下去，大部队在天黑之前应该是进不了城了。在这里下马吧，我们等一等身后的人。

The scouting unit clearing the way for the march dismounts on a gentle slope, taking shelter in the shade of a tree.
为大军开路的斥候小队在一处有阴凉遮蔽的缓坡边下了马。

Marsha: Careful, Merel. You'll break a leg if you dismount like that. Here, take my hand.
玛尔纱：梅蕾尔，小心，你这样下马会扭断腿的。扶住我。

Having already dismounted, Marsha walks over and helps Merel off her horse.
率先落地的玛尔纱来到被困在马上的梅蕾尔身边，扶她下马。

Merel: Th-Thank you, Marsha. Finally a bit of a break.
梅蕾尔：……谢、谢谢，玛尔纱，终于可以休息一会儿了。

Marsha: Sergey, the sun will go down before we arrive? Then, we'll be transitioning to a night march.
玛尔纱：谢尔盖，你刚刚说天黑之后才能进城？这么说，我们有一截路是要在夜里走。

Sergey: Yeah. We were supposed to put up for a night in Tamarovka and leave at dawn. What kind of army starts a march in the afternoon?
谢尔盖：是啊，按照计划，原本是在红柳树村过一夜，第二天清早出发。谁家进城是下午出门的？

Marsha: Night marches come with considerable dangers, especially when we don't know the enemy's movements. What's got the commander so anxious?
玛尔纱：在摸不清敌人动向的情况下，夜间行军是一件相当危险的事。军长为什么这么着急？

Sergey: He's a commander. He must have his reasons. Believe me. I know what it takes to lead a troop.
谢尔盖：当将军的，要考虑的事情可比我们要多多了，我知道当将军是怎么回事。

Sergey: My great-grandfather was a general, you know. Fought off Napoleon side by side with the Tsar himself! Came home with a chest of medals, too. Shame my family lost them.
谢尔盖：我的曾祖父就是一个大将军，和沙皇一起打过拿破仑，还往家里带回了一大堆勋章，只不过后来都弄丢了。

Without the taunts of his townsfolk, there's no one to rein in Sergey's bragging.
在没有同乡嘲笑的情况下，谢尔盖吹起牛来有些上不封顶了。

Sergey: If you ask me, it's because of the Timekeeper. The sooner she finishes up whatever it is she wants to do in the city, the sooner he can send her on her way.
谢尔盖：唉，真要说，估计是想早点把基金会老爷的事给安排妥当，好把她送走吧。

Sergey voices his opinion freely, not realizing that the person in front of him is a member of the Foundation.
谢尔盖大大咧咧地发表着猜想，丝毫没留意到面前的人就隶属于基金会。

Sergey: Then we won't be stuck guarding the city, and we can finally show the bandits what's what. You know how dangerous they are.
谢尔盖：等那个司辰一走，骑兵军也就不用把自己困在城里了。你也知道，玛尔纱，顿河的土匪不解决不行啊。

Merel: What's the Timekeeper doing here?
梅蕾尔：真想知道司辰到这种地方来做什么。

Marsha: You could always ask her. Aren't the two of you around the same age? Wouldn't you have been classmates at school?
玛尔纱：你也可以去问问司辰——说起来，你和她是一个学校的，年纪也差不多，为什么你和她不是同学？

Merel: Seriously? Can't we just drop this? Vertin was already the Timekeeper by the time I got there. I never saw her in school.
梅蕾尔：还是别提这回事了……在我入学的那一年，维尔汀已经是司辰了，她根本不在学校里。

Marsha: The SPDM—a school full of arcanists. It should be an interesting place.
玛尔纱：第一防线学校——全都是神秘学家的学校，那应该是个很有意思的地方。

Merel: Not as much as you'd think.
梅蕾尔：其实也没那么有意思。

Merel: Everything we learnt was "for the benefit of humanity" and "the supreme mission of human survival."
梅蕾尔：虽然全都是神秘学家小孩，但每天都教些“为人类利益服务”“人类存续至高使命”什么的东西……

Merel: Every day, there's a morning broadcast of the school motto in 12 languages, and the Student Handbook's thicker than the Oxford Dictionary.
梅蕾尔：早上6点起床的广播是用十二种语言播放校训，《学生守则》比《牛津词典》还要厚。

Merel: Oh, and the fence? It's electrocuted! Can you imagine? 230 volts of electricity buzzing day and night! It's more like a prison than a school.
梅蕾尔：简直就是监狱——学校的围墙上甚至有电网，你能想象吗——230伏！不间断地滋滋响。

Merel: There was a rumor back then that a group of students managed to escape. People said it was the Timekeeper who organized it.
梅蕾尔：倒是想起个事，学校里流传着曾有学生集体“越狱”——传说那个司辰就是“越狱”行动的主谋。

Marsha: The Timekeeper? That's difficult to imagine. She seems very level-headed.
玛尔纱：司辰？这倒是看不出来，她看起来总是很平静。

Merel: People were kind of scared of her. Apparently she was their leader.
梅蕾尔：她挺可怕的，听说她就是个孩子王。

Marsha: Did she or the other children bully you?
玛尔纱：怎么，你或者其他同学受过她的欺负吗？

Merel freezes at the question.
梅蕾尔怔住了。

Merel: Not at all. Why do you ask?
梅蕾尔：当然没有，为什么要这么问？

Marsha: I was bullied by someone of that type when I was in the Order.
玛尔纱：骑士团里也有一些“孩子王”，我还小的时候就被他们欺负过。

Marsha speaks as if it were nothing more than a passage from a textbook.
玛尔纱轻巧地说着，像在描述课本里的内容，而不是一段难以启齿的回忆。

Marsha: I was about half the age I am now. As an arcanist, my physical fitness was poor before I received training from the Order.
玛尔纱：那时候我的年龄还不到现在的一半，作为神秘学家，在接受正式的骑士团训练之前，体能也非常差。

Marsha: Some older squires would give newcomers like me a hard time. We didn't stand a chance.
玛尔纱：一些年纪比较大的侍从经常会为难我这样的新人，我们根本不是他们的对手。

Merel: What's a squire?
梅蕾尔：侍从是什么？

Marsha: They serve the knights and are usually candidates for knighthood. Newcomers take on menial tasks while they study the arcane skills of the Order. Those who master them become squires.
玛尔纱：为骑士服务的人，通常也是骑士的候选人。
新人会从杂役做起，学习骑士团的神秘术。掌握熟练的人可以成为侍从。

Merel: Wow, and you ended up a knight! Did you train thinking you'd go to war?
梅蕾尔：而你最后当上了骑士——真厉害。你在训练的时候就想着要上战场吗？

Marsha: War ... I had always pictured it as something far off—a legendary battle of dreams and ideals.
玛尔纱：在战争爆发之前，我都觉得战争只是一个遥远的、光鲜亮丽的神话。

Marsha: Until it broke out. I'm no longer so naive.
玛尔纱：人们带着守护理想的信念才踏上战场……不过现在我已经不会这么天真了。

Merel: ...
梅蕾尔：……

Merel: You must've taught those bullies of yours a lesson, right?
梅蕾尔：那些曾经欺负过你的人，后来一定被你教训得很惨吧？

Marsha's mouth curls into a mysterious smile.
红发骑士神秘莫测地笑了笑。

Marsha: *chuckle* Very few squires are granted knighthood.
玛尔纱：骑士团中只有非常、非常少的侍从有资格晋升为骑士。

Sergey, who has been listening along, cuts in.
一直在旁听的哥萨克打断了二人的谈话。

Sergey: Alright, rest's over. Back in your saddles. Better not let the team see us lounging around.
谢尔盖：好了，我看也休息够了。出发吧，可别让最前排的队伍看见我们几个坐在这儿休息。

Sergey straightens up and checks that the saddles are securely fastened. The others follow suit.
谢尔盖抖擞精神，起身检查了一遍马鞍的肚带是否牢固。小队中的其他人也跟着行动起来。

Sergey: You'll see Bereggrad once we reach the top of the slope.
谢尔盖：翻过眼前这道坡，我们就能看见别列格勒城了。

He rides slowly toward the top.
他策马缓步向坡顶走去。

Sergey: Those were some stories. Didn't have anything half as exciting in my childhood. By the way, I can show you around the city if you've got time. I know it well.
谢尔盖：你们说的事可真新鲜，我小时候就无聊多了。对了，有空的话，进城之后我带你们逛逛，我可是熟悉得很。

Sergey: There's a Turkish place that makes the best kebabs! Ah, but they might've fled already.
谢尔盖：我知道一家土耳其人开的饭店，烤肉的味道是真不错！唉……就是不知道他是不是逃难去了……

Leading the way, Sergey quickly reaches the top of the slope.
作为领头人，谢尔盖很快来到了坡顶。

Sergey: Behold! The Don's great—What?
谢尔盖：你们看，这就是——什么？

Sergey pulls hard on the reins, stopping his horse just before the crest.
谢尔盖猛拉缰绳，在快要越过坡顶时勒住了马。

Marsha: What's wrong?
玛尔纱：怎么了？

Sergey: Wait. Am I seeing things? Why are there so many people outside the city?
谢尔盖：不，是我眼花了？别列格勒城下怎么有……这么多人？

They ride up to his side. A dark crowd forms a wall across the Cavalry Corps's route to Bereggrad.
身后人纷纷来到坡顶。在骑兵军的必经之路上，横亘着一道黑压压的人墙。

Marsha: This is an army. They're blocking the entrance.
玛尔纱：是一支……军队，他们拦住了通往别列格勒的道路。

Sergey: What? At this time of day?
谢尔盖：怎、怎么会在这个时候——

Marsha: Sergey, tell the commander to halt the march. Merel and I will stay and observe their movements. Go!
玛尔纱：谢尔盖，回去告诉军长停止行军，我和梅蕾尔留在这里观察城下军队的动向。快去！

Sergey: A-Alright!
谢尔盖：好……好！

Commander Ivan: Outside Bereggrad? Did you get a clear look? Bandits or other forces?
军长伊万：别列格勒城下？——你看清他们的样子了吗？
是匪军，还是别的什么军队？

Sergey: I ... I'm not sure. But they're definitely not bandits. There are too many of them and ... I think I saw armored cars.
谢尔盖：我……不太看得清，但我敢肯定不是匪军。他们绝对不会有那么多人的，而且……我好像还看到了装甲车。

Sergey: Oh, and a banner, with a gryphon on it.
谢尔盖：对了，还有一面旗帜，上面有狮鹫的图案。

Commander Ivan: A gryphon ...
军长伊万：狮鹫……

The commander's brow darkens.
军长止住了接下来的话。

Commander Ivan: Hold all regiments. Halt the advance and take the high ground outside Bereggrad. Do not engage.
军长伊万：你和其他传令兵一起去通知各团停止前进，在别列格勒城外的高地上结成防线。但是不要主动与敌军交火。

Sergey: Yes, Commander.
谢尔盖：是，军长。

The temporary liaison rushes off. The commander turns to the solemn chief of staff and Flying Witch captain at his side.
临时传令官飞奔而去。
军长转向身侧神色凝重的参谋长，以及率领空中力量的飞行女巫。

Sternova: A gryphon ...
斯捷尔诺娃：狮鹫旗帜。

Commander Ivan: Igor's banner.
军长伊万：……是伊戈尔本人使用的军旗。

Sternova: So he's here, just outside Bereggrad, only half a day's march away.
斯捷尔诺娃：这么说他就在顿河，就在别列格勒城下，在离我们不过半天路程的地方。

Sternova: Our orders are to enter Bereggrad. If Igor blocks our way or tries to attack, we have reason to engage.
斯捷尔诺娃：我们得到的命令是前往别列格勒，如果伊戈尔拦住了我们的去路或是展露敌意，我们有理由对他发动进攻。

Commander Ivan: But the city and its people may already be under his thumb.
军长伊万：但别列格勒城以及城里的居民正在他的控制之下。

Commander Ivan: If the upstream villages truly were pillaged by his hand, then he may threaten us with the lives of Bereggrad's people.
军长伊万：如果上游村庄发生的那些事都是伊戈尔所为，那么他也会用城市里的生命作为威胁，阻止我们前进。

The commander's hand instinctively reaches for the gun at his waist. The cold metal calms his nerves.
军长不自觉搭上了腰间的佩枪，冰冷的金属触感令他稍微冷静了一点。

Commander Ivan: Lieutenant Lilya, your unit will confirm the number and formation of Igor's army.
军长伊万：红弩箭中尉，我需要你的人从空中确认伊戈尔军的数量和具体部署。

Commander Ivan: Retreat if attacked. Suffer no losses.
军长伊万：遇到防空火力就尽快撤退，尽量不要有损失。

Lilya: On it.
红弩箭：交给我吧。

15  向新日致意 / Salute to the New Sun

Sentinel Unit Internal Notice No. 47
近卫团内部通告 第47号

To all Sentinel Unit members:
    Intelligence confirmed that former chief of staff Captain Krutov deserted during the return voyage from the Antarctic Operation, taking critical documents and military equipment, including classified files and multiple communication devices.
    His whereabouts remain unknown. It is highly likely that he has defected to operate as an independent force or has entered into collaboration with Manus Vindictae.
    Admiral Igor has designated him as a top-priority threat. Any personnel who encounter Krutov during field missions or transit must report so immediately. Do not pursue or take unauthorized action.

    Additionally, according to the latest announcement from headquarters, Admiral Igor will personally lead elite forces to the Bereggrad region in the lower Don after completion of the Sanctuary's infrastructure. This operation involves coordination between the front line and the intelligence department. To prevent communication leaks, subsequent personnel assignments will be communicated under separate notice.

    Finally, in light of ongoing organizational restructuring, the Sentinel Unit will be renamed the "Gryphon Legion."
    We welcome alternative naming proposals. If you wish to submit one, please deliver your suggestion to Lieutenant Moldir within seven days.
    Note: Names must reflect our military ethos. Offensive, religious, or politically suggestive terms are prohibited.

Issued by: Lieutenant Moldir
Date: ██ ██
各位近卫团成员：
经确认，原参谋长格鲁托夫上尉在南极行动结束后，于返航途中擅自离队并带走部分重要资料及军事装备，包括机密文件与通讯设备若干。
其去向不明，极有可能已经叛变为独立势力，或与重塑之手达成新的合作。
目前，伊戈尔将军已将其列为最高潜在危险个体。任何人若在外勤任务或转运途中与格鲁托夫发生接触，应立即报告，不得擅自追踪或采取多余行动。 

此外，根据指挥部最新通报，伊戈尔将军将于“庇护所”的基础设施建设完成后，亲率精锐部队前往顿河下游的别列格勒地区执行任务。本次任务涉及前线与情报部门的两线协作，为防止通讯泄露，后续人员调派由我本人另行通知。

最后，鉴于组织职能与结构的逐步调整，近卫团拟更名为“狮鹫军”。
当然，我们同样欢迎并接受其他取名建议。如有意提议，请于7日内将更名方案交给莫莉德尔副官。
注：除不得使用侮辱性、宗教性或政治暗示性词汇外，名称应符合我军精神面貌。

签发人：莫莉德尔中尉
签发时间：██月██日

16  谈判者，审判者 / The Negotiators and the Judge

The Negotiators and the Judge
谈判者，审判者

A shallow valley separates the two armies.
一道开阔的浅谷远远分隔开两支迎面相望的大军。

The wind stirs seeds and dust. The warhorses grow restless under the eerie silence.
大陆的风卷起草籽与尘埃，骑兵胯下的战马在这份奇异的宁静之下焦躁不安。

Zeno Officer I: I'll be damned. It's really him—Igor. After all this time, he just shows up here in Bereggrad? What's he trying to do, exactly?
芝诺军官Ⅰ：该死的，真的是他。伊戈尔，找了他这么久，居然在别列格勒冒出来了。他到底想做什么？

Zeno Officer II: Whatever it is, he should do it soon so we can end him. I bet he wants to take his chances against us too.
芝诺军官Ⅱ：不管想做什么，但愿他快动手吧，这样我们就能狠狠揍他一顿了。我看他看我们也不顺眼。

Zeno Officer III: Not so fast. You know Igor parted ways with Manus Vindictae after the Antarctic operation.
芝诺军官Ⅲ：也未必，上校，你不也知道吗？在南极行动之后，伊戈尔和重塑之手就不是一路人了。

Zeno Officer I: Are you on that traitor's side?
芝诺军官Ⅰ：你替一个叛徒说什么话？

Zeno Officer II: He parted ways with us too!
芝诺军官Ⅱ：那也和我们不是一路人！

The roar of broom engines on their return flight briefly drowns out the quarrel below.
返航飞行扫帚的引擎声短暂地盖过了地面上的争执。

Zeno Officer III: Alright, alright! Quit your bickering! The flight unit's back.
芝诺军官Ⅲ：够了，够了！别吵了，飞行中队返航了。

Zeno Officer III: It's all up to the commander now.
芝诺军官Ⅲ：接下来要怎么做，只有军长能做决定。

The commander lowers his telescope as he awaits the Flying Witches' news.
军长放下望远镜，等待着带来消息的飞行女巫从空中降落。

Lilya emerges from a whirl of dust stirred up by the Su-02ве.
红弩箭出现在Su-02ве尾流扬起的一阵尘土中。

Commander Ivan: Status?
军长伊万：情况如何？

Lilya: It's weird. It's Igor all right, Triple-As and all, but they didn't attack.
红弩箭：怪事。我看见伊戈尔的军队里明明有防空火力，但没有对我们的侦察造成干扰。

Lilya: I flew low, too, even looked eye-to-eye with the soldiers, but they still held fire.
红弩箭：我还专门飞到很低的地方，几乎能和地面的士兵对上眼，但他们就是没有向我们开火。

Lilya: As for the city, there's no signs of damage or Igor's men.
红弩箭：至于城内，我暂时没看到有遭到破坏的迹象，而且伊戈尔也没有向城市里大规模派遣军队。

Sternova: His soldiers are all outside the city?
斯捷尔诺娃：他把所有人都安排在城外？

Lilya: Yeah. Got his main force positioned along the main road to it. It's like he wants us to see him.
红弩箭：没错，主力部队还在通往城市的主路一旁，就好像是刻意要让我们看见。

Commander Ivan: He wants us to see him.
军长伊万：刻意让我们看见……

Lilya: If he wanted to stop our advance, capturing the city would've been the best bet.
红弩箭：嗯，如果他们想阻止骑兵军前进，直接占领城市也是个好选择。但显然伊戈尔没有这么做。

Lilya: It's almost like he's welcoming us.
红弩箭：他就像是专门在这儿等着和我们见面。

Commander Ivan: ...
军长伊万：……

The commander raises his telescope again. This time, he spots a soldier riding out from Igor's army.
军长再次拿起望远镜观察对方军中的动向，这次，他看见一名士兵策马从伊戈尔军中脱离。

Commander Ivan: What?
军长伊万：什么？

The messenger crosses the ground between the two forces, drawing closer to the cavalry. His rebel uniform causes quite a stir.
信使穿越两军对峙中的空地，逐渐接近骑兵军。他身上的叛军制服引起了不小的骚动。

Lilya: That's—
红弩箭：是他——！

Commander Ivan: Calm yourself, Lieutenant. A rider must assess the terrain before he takes to a gallop.
军长伊万：先别急，中尉，让我们听听他带来了什么消息。

The commander holds fire. The messenger reaches the cavalry formation and approaches the commander, escorted by a guard.
军长按下了可能的擦枪走火。信使接近了骑兵军的军阵，随后在警卫连士兵的伴随下来到了军长面前。

Ptolemy: Commander Ivan Ivanovich. It's been a while.
托勒密：伊万·伊万诺维奇军长，好久不见。

Commander Ivan: A long time indeed. What message do you bring, Ptolemy?
军长伊万：好久不见，托勒密。你给我带来了什么消息？

The messenger stares straight into the commander's eyes, intent on carrying out the mission entrusted to him by his father.
叛军信使直直盯着军长，认真履行着“父亲”派下的任务。

Ptolemy: Admiral Igor wishes to talk with you and the Timekeeper, who's currently under your protection.
托勒密：伊戈尔将军想与您，以及目前受您保护的司辰会谈。

Ptolemy: There's something important he wants to discuss, about Bereggrad, the Don, and the Timekeeper's mission.
托勒密：他有很重要的事情要与二位商讨——关于别列格勒，关于顿河，关于……司辰来别列格勒的使命。

Vertin: Igor has the information I need.
维尔汀：……伊戈尔掌握着我需要的消息。

Commander Ivan: Seems he knows why you're here. Though this could be a ploy.
军长伊万：看来他知道你为什么到顿河来，当然，这也可能只是他的骗局。

Sonetto: Hmm. It's too dangerous for the Timekeeper to meet Igor in person. They should speak via communication device instead.
十四行诗：让司辰和伊戈尔见面太危险了。我们可以和对方交换通讯设备，代替面谈。

Vertin: They could easily have reached us through such devices. Given this message was brought by one of his most trusted subordinates ...
维尔汀：如果伊戈尔提出要和我以及军长面谈，那这个条件应该是不能退让的。更何况他派了自己绝对的亲信来传递口信。

Vertin: I don't think it's negotiable.
维尔汀：不然，想用无线设备联系到我们应该并不困难。

Vertin: What are your thoughts, Commander?
维尔汀：你怎么看，军长？

The commander finds himself the most unsure of his decision.
面对众人，军长发现自己是最难做出决定的那一个。

Commander Ivan: I want to know his purpose here, and him stationing outside the city does look like a gesture of goodwill.
军长伊万：我的确很想知道他到底在顿河做什么。况且，他把军队停在城下，似乎也是一种坦率的表现……

Commander Ivan: That said, I shouldn't meet him in private. Word will surely reach the Joint Committee.
军长伊万：但是，从原则上说，我不应该和叛军首领私下见面。这件事情无法隐瞒，一定会被联合委员会知晓。

Vertin: I understand your concerns. But either way, I'll meet him.
维尔汀：我明白你的担忧。不过，至少我是会去见伊戈尔的。

Sonetto: Timekeeper?!
十四行诗：——司辰？！

Vertin: I currently have almost no information on my mission. I'd like to know what he has to say, even if it's a lie.
维尔汀：我目前对任务需要的情报几乎一无所知，我想听听他会说什么，哪怕是个骗局。

The commander pauses to consider.
军长凝思片刻。

Commander Ivan: Our orders are to avoid confrontation. Accepting his request may be the way to do that. But ...
军长伊万：我得到的命令是尽量不与伊戈尔发生冲突，那么接受会面也是完成命令的一种方式，只不过……

Commander Ivan: Whether I join the meeting is your decision.
军长伊万：我是否参与会面只能你来做决定，司辰。

Vertin: Me?
维尔汀：我？

Commander Ivan: The Committee will not question your motivations. If I attend, let it be by your bidding.
军长伊万：委员会不会质疑你与伊戈尔见面的动机，所以，如果我要参与会面，最好是你来决定，而我配合你的行动。

Commander Ivan: Then, if they question me, I will have my defense.
军长伊万：只有这样，事后如果有针对此事的问询，我才有为自己辩护的空间。

Vertin: I see. I'll make the decision.
维尔汀：……好的，我接受这个方案。

Sonetto: But how can we ensure the safety of the Timekeeper and the others?
十四行诗：可是，我们要如何保证司辰和各位的安全？

Lilya: Meeting Igor, huh? Take me with you, Vertin. I'd like to hear what he's got to say.
红弩箭：见伊戈尔？这我得和你一起去，头儿，我真想听听他要说些什么。

Vertin: Alright. I accept Igor's invitation, and I request the commander join me.
维尔汀：那么，我同意伊戈尔的会面要求，军长也要与我一同前往。

Vertin: To ensure the safety of both parties, I suggest we each bring our own bodyguards. Lilya will be ours.
维尔汀：不过，红弩箭要作为护卫参加会谈，而伊戈尔也可以带上自己的护卫，保障双方的安全。

Sonetto: You could use another, Timekeeper.
十四行诗：您的护卫还可以增加一人，司辰。
